[ 109] // POST /convert/pdf-to-image
[ 110] // Convertit un PDF en images JPG ou PNG via poppler-utils (pdftoppm)
[ 111] // Chaque page du PDF devient une image séparée
[ 112] // On retourne un fichier ZIP contenant toutes les images
[ 113] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 114] router.post('/pdf-to-image', upload.single('file'), (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[ 115] 
[ 116]     if (!req.file) {
[ 117]         return res.status(400).json({ message: 'Aucun fichier reçu.' });
[ 118]     }
[ 119] 
[ 120]     // Format demandé par l'utilisateur — jpg par défaut
[ 121]     const format = req.query.format === 'png' ? 'png' : 'jpeg';
[ 122]     const inputPath = req.file.path;
[ 123] 
[ 124]     // Préfixe pour les fichiers de sortie — pdftoppm ajoute -1, -2, -3...
[ 125]     const outputPrefix = `/tmp/${Date.now()}-page`;
[ 126] 
[ 127]     // Gestion de la qualité (HD = 300 DPI, Standard = 150 DPI)
[ 128]     const quality = req.body.quality || 'standard';
[ 129]     const dpi = quality === 'hd' ? 300 : 150;
[ 130] 
[ 131]     // Commande pdftoppm pour convertir toutes les pages en images
[ 132]     const command = `pdftoppm -${format === 'jpeg' ? 'jpeg' : 'png'} -r ${dpi} "${inputPath}" "${outputPrefix}"`;
[ 133] 
[ 134]     exec(command, (error, stdout, stderr) => {
[ 135]         if (error) {
[ 136]             console.error("pdftoppm error:", error, stderr);
[ 137]             return res.status(500).json({ message: 'La conversion a échoué. Veuillez réessayer.', error: error.message, stderr: stderr });
[ 138]         }
[ 139] 
[ 140]         // On liste toutes les images générées par pdftoppm
[ 141]         const files = fs.readdirSync('/tmp').filter(f => f.startsWith(path.basename(outputPrefix)));
[ 142] 
[ 143]         if (files.length === 0) {
[ 144]             return res.status(500).json({ message: 'Aucune image générée.' });
[ 145]         }
[ 146] 
[ 147]         // Si une seule page → on retourne directement l'image
[ 148]         if (files.length === 1) {
[ 149]             const imagePath = `/tmp/${files[0]}`;
[ 150]             res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="page-1.${format === 'jpeg' ? 'jpg' : 'png'}"`);
[ 151]             res.setHeader('Content-Type', `image/${format}`);
[ 152]             res.download(imagePath, (err) => {
[ 153]         if (fs.existsSync(imagePath)) {
[ 154]             try { fs.unlinkSync(imagePath); } catch(e) {}
[ 155]         }
[ 156]     });
[ 157]             
[ 158]             return;
[ 159]         }
[ 160] 
[ 161]         // Si plusieurs pages → on crée un ZIP avec toutes les images
[ 162]         const zipPath = `/tmp/${Date.now()}-images.zip`;
[ 163]         const fileList = files.map(f => `/tmp/${f}`).join(' ');
[ 164]         exec(`zip ${zipPath} ${fileList}`, (zipError) => {
[ 165]             if (zipError) {
[ 166]                 return res.status(500).json({ message: 'Erreur lors de la création du ZIP.' });
[ 167]             }
[ 168] 
[ 169]             res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', 'attachment; filename="images.zip"');
[ 170]             res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/zip');
[ 171]             res.download(zipPath, (err) => {
[ 172]         if (fs.existsSync(zipPath)) {
[ 173]             try { fs.unlinkSync(zipPath); } catch(e) {}
[ 174]         }
[ 175]     });
[ 176] 
[ 177]             // On supprime tous les fichiers temporaires
[ 178]             
[ 179]             files.forEach(f => { try { fs.unlinkSync(`/tmp/${f}`); } catch(e) {} });
[ 180]         });
[ 181]     });
[ 182] 
[ 183] });
[ 184]

[ 387] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 388] // POST /convert/split-pdf
[ 389] // Divise un PDF en plusieurs fichiers (un par page)
[ 390] // Retourne un ZIP contenant tous les PDFs
[ 391] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 392] router.post('/split-pdf', upload.single('file'), async (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[ 393]     if (!req.file) return res.status(400).json({ message: 'Aucun fichier reçu.' });
[ 394] 
[ 395]     try {
[ 396]         const splitPdfs = await splitPDF(req.file.path);
[ 397]         const zipPath = `/tmp/${Date.now()}-split.zip`;
[ 398] 
[ 399]         // On sauvegarde chaque PDF temporairement puis on zippe
[ 400]         const tempFiles = [];
[ 401]         const timestamp = Date.now();
[ 402]         const splitMode = req.body.splitMode || 'all';
[ 403] 
[ 404]         for (let i = 0; i < splitPdfs.length; i++) {
[ 405]             const pageNumber = i + 1;
[ 406]             const isEven = pageNumber % 2 === 0;
[ 407] 
[ 408]             if (splitMode === 'even' && !isEven) continue;
[ 409]             if (splitMode === 'odd' && isEven) continue;
[ 410] 
[ 411]             const tempPath = `/tmp/${timestamp}-page-${pageNumber}.pdf`;
[ 412]             fs.writeFileSync(tempPath, splitPdfs[i]);
[ 413]             tempFiles.push(tempPath);
[ 414]         }
[ 415] 
[ 416]         const fileList = tempFiles.join(' ');
[ 417]         exec(`zip ${zipPath} ${fileList}`, (error) => {
[ 418]             if (error) return res.status(500).json({ message: 'Erreur lors de la création du ZIP.' });
[ 419] 
[ 420]             res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', 'attachment; filename="pages.zip"');
[ 421]             res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/zip');
[ 422]             res.download(zipPath, (err) => {
[ 423]         if (fs.existsSync(zipPath)) {
[ 424]             try { fs.unlinkSync(zipPath); } catch(e) {}
[ 425]         }
[ 426]     });
[ 427] 
[ 428]             try { fs.unlinkSync(req.file.path); } catch(e) {}
[ 429]             tempFiles.forEach(p => { try { fs.unlinkSync(p); } catch(e) {} });
[ 430]         });
[ 431]     } catch (error) {
[ 432]         res.status(500).json({ message: 'La division a échoué. Veuillez réessayer.' });
[ 433]     }
[ 434] });
[ 435]

[ 513]     if (!req.file) return res.status(400).json({ message: 'Aucun fichier reçu.' });

[ 522] 
[ 523]     exec(command, (error) => {
[ 524]         if (error) return res.status(500).json({ message: 'La protection a échoué. Veuillez réessayer.' });
[ 525] 
[ 526]         res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', 'attachment; filename="protected.pdf"');
[ 527]         res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/pdf');
[ 528]         res.download(outputPath, (err) => {
[ 529]         if (fs.existsSync(outputPath)) {
[ 530]             try { fs.unlinkSync(outputPath); } catch(e) {}
[ 531]         }
[ 532]     });
[ 533] 
[ 534]         
[ 535]     });

[ 586] // POST /convert/ai-summarize
[ 587] // Résume le contenu d'un PDF en utilisant l'IA Anthropic (Claude)
[ 588] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 589] router.post('/ai-summarize', upload.single('file'), async (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[ 590]     if (!req.file) return res.status(400).json({ message: 'Aucun fichier reçu.' });
[ 591] 
[ 592]     try {
[ 593]         const text = await extractTextFromPDF(req.file.path, 'fra');
[ 594]         
[ 595]         let summaryText = "";
[ 596]         
[ 597]         if (!process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) {
[ 598]             summaryText = "Résumé (MOCK - Pas de clé API Anthropic configurée) :\n\n" + text.substring(0, 1000) + "\n\n[Fin du résumé mocké]";
[ 599]         } else {
[ 600]             const Anthropic = require('@anthropic-ai/sdk');
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[ 601]             const anthropic = new Anthropic({
[ 602]                 apiKey: process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
[ 603]             });
[ 604]             const response = await anthropic.messages.create({
[ 605]                 model: "claude-3-haiku-20240307",
[ 606]                 max_tokens: 1024,
[ 607]                 messages: [
[ 608]                     { role: "user", content: "Fais un résumé détaillé du texte suivant :\n\n" + text }
[ 609]                 ]
[ 610]             });
[ 611]             summaryText = response.content[0].text;
[ 612]         }
[ 613] 
[ 614]         res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', 'attachment; filename="resume.txt"');
[ 615]         res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain');
[ 616]         res.send(summaryText);
[ 617] 
[ 618]         try { fs.unlinkSync(req.file.path); } catch(e) {}
[ 619]     } catch (error) {
[ 620]         res.status(500).json({ message: 'L\'IA a échoué. ' + error.message });
[ 621]     }
[ 622] });

[ 917] router.post('/compress-pdf', upload.single('file'), (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[ 918]     if (!req.file) {
[ 919]         return res.status(400).send('Aucun fichier fourni.');
[ 920]     }
[ 921] 
[ 922]     const level = req.body.compressionLevel || 'recommended';
[ 923]     let pdfSettings = '/ebook'; // recommended
[ 924] 
[ 925]     if (level === 'extreme') {
[ 926]         pdfSettings = '/screen';
[ 927]     } else if (level === 'low') {
[ 928]         pdfSettings = '/printer';
[ 929]     }
[ 930] 
[ 931]     const jobId = Date.now().toString() + '-' + Math.round(Math.random() * 1000);
[ 932]     const safeInputPath = `/tmp/job-input-${jobId}.pdf`;
[ 933]     const outputPath = `/tmp/job-output-${jobId}.pdf`;
[ 934] 
[ 935]     // 1. Déplacer le fichier pour éviter sa suppression par le middleware global
[ 936]     // Utiliser copyFileSync + unlinkSync au lieu de renameSync pour éviter les erreurs EXDEV (fichiers sur partitions différentes)
[ 937]     try {
[ 938]         fs.copyFileSync(req.file.path, safeInputPath);
[ 939]         fs.unlinkSync(req.file.path);
[ 940]     } catch (err) {
[ 941]         console.error("Erreur copie fichier :", err);
[ 942]         return res.status(500).json({ error: "Erreur serveur lors de la copie du fichier." });
[ 943]     }
[ 944] 
[ 945]     // 2. Enregistrer le job
[ 946]     jobs.set(jobId, { status: 'processing', progress: 0 });
[ 947] 
[ 948]     // 3. Répondre immédiatement avec le jobId
[ 949]     res.json({ jobId });
[ 950] 
[ 951]     // 4. Lancer Ghostscript en arrière-plan
[ 952]     const gsCommand = `gs -sDEVICE=pdfwrite -dCompatibilityLevel=1.4 -dPDFSETTINGS=${pdfSettings} -dNOPAUSE -dQUIET -dBATCH -sOutputFile="${outputPath}" "${safeInputPath}"`;
[ 953] 
[ 954]     exec(gsCommand, { maxBuffer: 1024 * 1024 * 10 }, (error) => {
[ 955]         // Nettoyer le fichier d'entrée
[ 956]         if (fs.existsSync(safeInputPath)) {
[ 957]             try { fs.unlinkSync(safeInputPath); } catch (e) {}
[ 958]         }
[ 959] 
[ 960]         if (error) {
[ 961]             console.error('Erreur Ghostscript compression:', error);
[ 962]             jobs.set(jobId, { status: 'error', error: 'Erreur lors de la compression du PDF.' });
[ 963]             return;
[ 964]         }
[ 965] 
[ 966]         jobs.set(jobId, { status: 'done', outputPath });
[ 967]         
[ 968]         // Optionnel : nettoyer le job et le fichier de sortie au bout de 10 minutes s'il n'a pas été téléchargé
[ 969]         setTimeout(() => {
[ 970]             if (jobs.has(jobId)) {
[ 971]                 jobs.delete(jobId);
[ 972]                 if (fs.existsSync(outputPath)) {
[ 973]                     try { fs.unlinkSync(outputPath); } catch (e) {}
[ 974]                 }
[ 975]             }
[ 976]         }, 10 * 60 * 1000);
[ 977]     });
[ 978] });
[ 979] 
[ 980] router.get('/status/:jobId', (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[ 981]     const jobId = req.params.jobId;
[ 982]     const job = jobs.get(jobId);
[ 983]     
[ 984]     if (!job) {
[ 985]         return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Job non trouvé' });

--- FICHIER : routes/expire.js (67 lignes) ---
Ce fichier est un pilier de l'architecture. Voici son contenu intégral avec documentation détaillée :

[   1] /**
[   2]  * routes/expire.js
[   3]  *
[   4]  * Gère la suppression manuelle des fichiers par le sender.
[   5]  * Le sender peut supprimer son fichier avant que quelqu'un
[   6]  * ne le télécharge.
[   7]  */
[   8] 
[   9] const express = require('express');
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  10] const supabase = require('../services/supabase');
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  11] const { s3Client } = require('../services/r2');
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  12] const { DeleteObjectCommand } = require('@aws-sdk/client-s3');
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  13] 
[  14] const router = express.Router();
[  15] 
[  16] // DELETE /expire/:id
[  17] router.delete('/:id', async (req, res) => {
[  18]     const { id } = req.params;
[  19] 
[  20]     try {
[  21]         const { data: transfer, error } = await supabase
[  22]             .from('transfers')
[  23]             .select('*')
[  24]             .eq('id', id)
[  25]             .single();
[  26] 
[  27]         if (error || !transfer) {
[  28]             return res.status(404).json({ message: 'Ce transfert n\'existe plus ou a déjà été supprimé.' });
[  29]         }
[  30] 
[  31]         if (transfer.downloaded) {
[  32]             return res.status(200).json({ message: 'Ce transfert a déjà été téléchargé et supprimé.' });
[  33]         }
[  34] 
[  35]         // Suppression R2 (Gestion Single et Batch)
[  36]         if (transfer.file_url.startsWith('[')) {
[  37]             const files = JSON.parse(transfer.file_url);
[  38]             for (const file of files) {
[  39]                 const command = new DeleteObjectCommand({
[  40]                     Bucket: process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME,
[  41]                     Key: file.storageName
[  42]                 });
[  43]                 await s3Client.send(command).catch(e => console.error('Erreur suppression R2 lot:', e));
[  44]             }
[  45]         } else {
[  46]             const command = new DeleteObjectCommand({
[  47]                 Bucket: process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME,
[  48]                 Key: transfer.file_url
[  49]             });
[  50]             await s3Client.send(command).catch(e => console.error('Erreur suppression R2 single:', e));
[  51]         }
[  52] 
[  53]         // Suppression des métadonnées
[  54]         await supabase
[  55]             .from('transfers')
[  56]             .delete()
[  57]             .eq('id', id);
[  58] 
[  59]         res.status(200).json({ message: 'Fichiers supprimés avec succès.' });
[  60] 
[  61]     } catch (err) {
[  62]         console.error('Exception dans /expire:', err);
[  63]         res.status(500).json({ message: 'Erreur interne du serveur lors de la suppression' });
[  64]     }
[  65] });
[  66] 
[  67] module.exports = router;

[  13] router.post('/request-url', async (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[  14]     try {
[  15]         const { fileName, contentType } = req.body;
[  16]         if (!fileName) {
[  17]             return res.status(400).json({ error: 'fileName manquant' });
[  18]         }
[  19] 
[  20]         const fileId = uuidv4();
[  21]         const cleanName = fileName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, '_');
[  22]         const storageName = `${fileId}-${cleanName}`;
[  23] 
[  24]         const command = new PutObjectCommand({

[  26]             Key: storageName,
[  27]             ContentType: contentType || 'application/octet-stream'
[  28]         });

[  35]             signedUrl
[  36]         });

[  43] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[  44] // POST /upload/request-urls (V2 - Batch Upload)
[  45] // Génère un ID de lot (batchId) et un ticket R2 pour chaque fichier
[  46] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[  47] router.post('/request-urls', async (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[  48]     try {
[  49]         const { files } = req.body; // array of { fileName, contentType }
[  50]         if (!files || !Array.isArray(files) || files.length === 0) {
[  51]             return res.status(400).json({ error: 'files manquant ou invalide' });
[  52]         }
[  53]         if (files.length > 500) {
[  54]             return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Trop de fichiers dans le lot (max 500)' });
[  55]         }
[  56] 
[  57]         const batchId = uuidv4();
[  58]         const uploadTickets = [];
[  59] 
[  60]         for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
[  61]             const { fileName, contentType } = files[i];
[  62]             const cleanName = fileName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, '_');
[  63]             const storageName = `${batchId}-${i}-${cleanName}`;
[  64] 
[  65]             const command = new PutObjectCommand({
[  66]                 Bucket: process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME,
[  67]                 Key: storageName,
[  68]                 ContentType: contentType || 'application/octet-stream'
[  69]             });
[  70] 
[  71]             const signedUrl = await getSignedUrl(s3Client, command, { expiresIn: 3600 });
[  72] 
[  73]             uploadTickets.push({
[  74]                 originalName: fileName,
[  75]                 storageName,
[  76]                 signedUrl
[  77]             });
[  78]         }
[  79] 
[  80]         res.status(200).json({
[  81]             batchId,
[  82]             uploadTickets
[  83]         });
[  84]     } catch (err) {
[  85]         console.error('Exception dans /request-urls R2:', err);
[  86]         res.status(500).json({ error: 'Erreur interne du serveur' });
[  87]     }

[ 105]         const command = new CreateMultipartUploadCommand({
[ 106]             Bucket: process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME,
[ 107]             Key: storageName,
[ 108]             ContentType: contentType || 'application/octet-stream'
[ 109]         });
[ 110] 
[ 111]         const response = await s3Client.send(command);
[ 112]         const uploadId = response.UploadId;
[ 113] 
[ 114]         // Pré-génération anti-bufferbloat
[ 115]         let presignedUrls = {};
[ 116]         if (totalParts && typeof totalParts === 'number') {
[ 117]             if (totalParts > 2000) {
[ 118]                 return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Nombre de parties trop élevé (max 2000)' });
[ 119]             }
[ 120]             for (let i = 1; i <= totalParts; i++) {
[ 121]                 const partCommand = new UploadPartCommand({
[ 122]                     Bucket: process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME,
[ 123]                     Key: storageName,
[ 124]                     UploadId: uploadId,
[ 125]                     PartNumber: i
[ 126]                 });
[ 127]                 presignedUrls[i] = await getSignedUrl(s3Client, partCommand, { expiresIn: 3600 * 24 }); // 24h au cas où
[ 128]             }
[ 129]         }
[ 130]         
[ 131]         res.status(200).json({
[ 132]             fileId,
[ 133]             storageName,
[ 134]             uploadId,
[ 135]             presignedUrls // Retourne le dictionnaire { 1: "url...", 2: "url..." }
[ 136]         });
[ 137]     } catch (err) {
[ 138]         console.error('Exception dans /multipart/start:', err);
[ 139]         res.status(500).json({ error: 'Erreur démarrage multipart' });
[ 140]     }
[ 141] });
[ 142] 
[ 143] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 144] // POST /upload/multipart/sign-part
[ 145] // Génère une URL présignée pour un chunk précis
[ 146] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 147] router.post('/multipart/sign-part', async (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[ 148]     try {
[ 149]         const { storageName, uploadId, partNumber } = req.body;
[ 150]         
[ 151]         if (!storageName || !uploadId || !partNumber) {
[ 152]             return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Paramètres manquants' });
[ 153]         }
[ 154] 
[ 155]         const command = new UploadPartCommand({
[ 156]             Bucket: process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME,
[ 157]             Key: storageName,
[ 158]             UploadId: uploadId,
[ 159]             PartNumber: partNumber
[ 160]         });
[ 161] 
[ 162]         const signedUrl = await getSignedUrl(s3Client, command, { expiresIn: 3600 });
[ 163]         
[ 164]         res.status(200).json({ signedUrl });
[ 165]     } catch (err) {
[ 166]         console.error('Exception dans /multipart/sign-part:', err);
[ 167]         res.status(500).json({ error: 'Erreur signature part' });
[ 168]     }
[ 169] });
[ 170] 
[ 171] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 172] // POST /upload/multipart/complete
[ 173] // Finalise l'upload multipart
[ 174] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 175] router.post('/multipart/complete', async (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[ 176]     try {
[ 177]         const { storageName, uploadId, parts } = req.body;
[ 178]         
[ 179]         if (!storageName || !uploadId || !parts) {
[ 180]             return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Paramètres manquants' });
[ 181]         }
[ 182] 
[ 183]         const command = new CompleteMultipartUploadCommand({
[ 184]             Bucket: process.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME,
[ 185]             Key: storageName,
[ 186]             UploadId: uploadId,
[ 187]             MultipartUpload: {
[ 188]                 Parts: parts // doit être un tableau de { ETag, PartNumber }
[ 189]             }
[ 190]         });
[ 191] 
[ 192]         await s3Client.send(command);
[ 193]         
[ 194]         res.status(200).json({ success: true });
[ 195]     } catch (err) {
[ 196]         console.error('Exception dans /multipart/complete:', err);
[ 197]         res.status(500).json({ error: 'Erreur finalisation multipart' });
[ 198]     }
[ 199] });
[ 200] 
[ 201] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 202] // POST /upload/confirm (Handles both Single and Batch modes)
[ 203] // ─────────────────────────────────────────────
[ 204] router.post('/confirm', async (req, res) => {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Définition d'un point d'entrée (Endpoint) API. Protégé par les middlewares.
[ 205]     try {
[ 206]         const { fileId, originalName, storageName, userId, files } = req.body;
[ 207]         
[ 208]         // Single file mode fallback vs Batch mode
[ 209]         if (!fileId) {
[ 210]             return res.status(400).json({ error: 'fileId manquant' });
[ 211]         }
[ 212] 
[ 213]         const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
[ 214]         let insertData = {
[ 215]             id: fileId,
[ 216]             expires_at: expiresAt,
[ 217]             downloaded: false
[ 218]         };
[ 219] 
[ 220]         if (files && Array.isArray(files) && files.length > 0) {
[ 221]             // Batch Mode (V2)
[ 222]             // files is expected to be an array of { originalName, storageName }
[ 223]             insertData.file_name = `Lot de ${files.length} fichiers`;
[ 224]             insertData.file_url = JSON.stringify(files); // Astuce: stocker le JSON dans la colonne text existante
[ 225]         } else if (originalName && storageName) {
[ 226]             // Single file Mode (V1)
[ 227]             insertData.file_name = originalName;
[ 228]             insertData.file_url = storageName;
[ 229]         } else {
[ 230]             return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Paramètres de fichier manquants (soit files, soit originalName+storageName)' });
[ 231]         }
[ 232] 
[ 233]         if (userId) {
[ 234]             insertData.user_id = userId;
[ 235]         }
[ 236] 
[ 237]         const { error: dbError } = await supabase
[ 238]             .from('transfers')
[ 239]             .insert(insertData);
[ 240] 
[ 241]         if (dbError) {
[ 242]             console.error('Erreur insertion confirm:', dbError);
[ 243]             return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Erreur lors de la sauvegarde des métadonnées' });
[ 244]         }
[ 245] 
[ 246]         res.status(201).json({
[ 247]             id: fileId,
[ 248]             downloadUrl: `https://faas-transfer.onrender.com/download/${fileId}`
[ 249]         });
[ 250]     } catch (err) {
[ 251]         console.error('Exception dans /confirm R2:', err);
[ 252]         res.status(500).json({ error: 'Erreur interne du serveur' });
[ 253]     }
[ 254] });
[ 255] 
[ 256] module.exports = router;
[ 257]

[  26] function RootContent() {

[  28]   const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);

[  30] 
[  31]   useEffect(() => {
[  32]     // Si on revient d'une connexion OAuth (Google/Microsoft), l'URL contient un token.
[  33]     // Il faut attendre que Supabase parse ce token et déclenche onAuthStateChange
[  34]     // avant d'arrêter le loader, sinon la page de connexion flashe à l'écran.
[  35]     const isOAuthRedirect = typeof window !== 'undefined' && 
[  36]       (window.location.hash.includes('access_token=') || window.location.hash.includes('error='));
[  37] 
[  38]     supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session } }) => {
[  39]       setSession(session);
[  40]       // On arrête le loader uniquement si ce n'est pas un retour OAuth
[  41]       // ou si on a déjà une session valide.
[  42]       if (!isOAuthRedirect || session) {
[  43]         setIsCheckingAuth(false);
[  44]       }
[  45]     });
[  46] 
[  47]     const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
[  48]       setSession(session);
[  49]       // Quand l'événement est reçu (ex: SIGNED_IN après OAuth), on arrête le loader
[  50]       setIsCheckingAuth(false);
[  51]     });
[  52]     
[  53]     return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
[  54]   }, []);
[  55] 
[  56]   if (isCheckingAuth) {
[  57]     return (
[  58]       <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center' }}>
[  59]         <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
[  60]       </View>
[  61]     );
[  62]   }
[  63] 
[  64]   if (!session) {

[  68]           <title>Fast Transfer - Connexion</title>
[  69]         </Head>
[  70]         <AuthScreen />
[  71]         <Toast />
[  72]       </NavigationThemeProvider>
[  73]     );
[  74]   }

[  78]       <Head>
[  79]         <title>Fast Transfer - Partage & Conversion de Fichiers Instantané</title>
[  80]         <meta name="description" content="Envoyez, recevez et convertissez vos fichiers (PDF, Vidéos, Audio, Images) instantanément et gratuitement. La solution tout-en-un pour vos transferts et traitements de documents." />
[  81]         <meta property="og:title" content="Fast Transfer - Partage & Conversion de Fichiers Instantané" />

[  11] export default function AuthScreen() {

[  40] const withRetry = async (fn: () => Promise<any>, retries = 3) => {
[  41]     let attempt = 0;
[  42]     while (attempt < retries) {
[  43]         try {
[  44]             return await fn();
[  45]         } catch (error) {
[  46]             attempt++;
[  47]             if (attempt >= retries) throw error;
[  48]             console.log(`Retry ${attempt}/${retries} failed, retrying in 2s...`);
[  49]             await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000));
[  50]         }
[  51]     }
[  52] };

[  93]     const formatSize = (bytes: number) => {
[  94]         if (!bytes || bytes === 0) return 'Taille inconnue';
[  95]         const k = 1024;
[  96]         const sizes = ['Octets', 'Ko', 'Mo', 'Go', 'To'];
[  97]         const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
[  98]         return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
[  99]     };

[ 144]     const pickFile = async (categoryId: string, mimeTypes: string[]) => {
[ 145]         setIsPicking(true);
[ 146]         setLastCategory(categoryId);
[ 147]         setLastMimeTypes(mimeTypes);
[ 148]         try {
[ 149]             let files: any[] = [];
[ 150] 
[ 151]             if (Platform.OS !== 'web' && (categoryId === 'images' || categoryId === 'video' || categoryId === 'audio')) {
[ 152]                 let mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaType = 'images';
[ 153]                 if (categoryId === 'video') mediaTypes = 'videos';
[ 154]                 
[ 155]                 if (categoryId === 'audio') {
[ 156]                     const res = await DocumentPicker.getDocumentAsync({
[ 157]                         type: mimeTypes,
[ 158]                         copyToCacheDirectory: false,
[ 159]                         multiple: true,
[ 160]                     });
[ 161]                     if (res.canceled) {
[ 162]                         setIsPicking(false);
[ 163]                         return;
[ 164]                     }
[ 165]                     files = res.assets;
[ 166]                 } else {
[ 167]                     const permissionResult = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
[ 168]                     if (permissionResult.granted === false) {
[ 169]                         Alert.alert("Permission requise", "Vous devez autoriser l'accès à vos photos.");
[ 170]                         setIsPicking(false);
[ 171]                         return;
[ 172]                     }
[ 173] 
[ 174]                     const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
[ 175]                         mediaTypes: mediaTypes,
[ 176]                         allowsMultipleSelection: true,
[ 177]                         preferredAssetRepresentationMode: ImagePicker.UIImagePickerPreferredAssetRepresentationMode.Current,
[ 178]                     });
[ 179] 
[ 180]                     if (result.canceled) {
[ 181]                         setIsPicking(false);
[ 182]                         return;
[ 183]                     }
[ 184]                     
[ 185]                     files = result.assets.map(asset => ({
[ 186]                         uri: asset.uri,
[ 187]                         name: asset.fileName || asset.uri.split('/').pop() || 'media.jpg',
[ 188]                         mimeType: asset.mimeType || (categoryId === 'video' ? 'video/mp4' : 'image/jpeg'),
[ 189]                         size: asset.fileSize || 0,
[ 190]                         file: (asset as any).file
[ 191]                     }));
[ 192]                 }
[ 193]             } else {
[ 194]                 const res = await DocumentPicker.getDocumentAsync({
[ 195]                     type: mimeTypes,
[ 196]                     copyToCacheDirectory: false,
[ 197]                     multiple: true,
[ 198]                 });
[ 199]                 if (res.canceled) {
[ 200]                     setIsPicking(false);
[ 201]                     return;
[ 202]                 }
[ 203]                 files = res.assets.map(asset => {
[ 204]                     let finalName = asset.name || (asset.file && (asset.file as any).name) || 'fichier';
[ 205]                     const mime = asset.mimeType || (asset.file && (asset.file as any).type) || '';
[ 206]                     if (mime.includes('video/') && !finalName.includes('.')) finalName += '.mp4';
[ 207]                     else if (mime.includes('image/') && !finalName.includes('.')) finalName += '.jpg';
[ 208]                     else if (mime.includes('audio/') && !finalName.includes('.')) finalName += '.mp3';
[ 209]                     
[ 210]                     return {
[ 211]                         ...asset,
[ 212]                         name: finalName,
[ 213]                         file: (asset as any).file
[ 214]                     };
[ 215]                 });
[ 216]             }
[ 217] 
[ 218]             if (!files || files.length === 0) {
[ 219]                 setIsPicking(false);
[ 220]                 return;
[ 221]             }
[ 222] 
[ 223]             setPendingFiles(prev => [...prev, ...files]);
[ 224]             setStep('review');
[ 225]             setIsPicking(false);
[ 226] 
[ 227]         } catch (error) {
[ 228]             setIsPicking(false);
[ 229]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') window.alert(t('common.error') + '\nImpossible de sélectionner le(s) fichier(s).');
[ 230]             else Alert.alert(t('common.error'), 'Impossible de sélectionner le(s) fichier(s).');
[ 231]         }
[ 232]     };
[ 233] 
[ 234]     const uploadFileLegacy = async (file: any) => {
[ 235]         setProgress(0);
[ 236]         setIsPreparing(false);
[ 237]         setStep('uploading');
[ 238] 
[ 239]         try {
[ 240]             const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
[ 241]             const reqUrlResponse = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/upload/request-url`, {
[ 242]                 method: 'POST',
[ 243]                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[ 244]                 body: JSON.stringify({
[ 245]                     fileName: file.name,
[ 246]                     contentType: file.mimeType || 'application/octet-stream',
[ 247]                     userId: session?.user?.id || null
[ 248]                 })
[ 249]             });
[ 250] 
[ 251]             if (!reqUrlResponse.ok) {
[ 252]                 throw new Error('Erreur lors de la demande de ticket au serveur');
[ 253]             }
[ 254] 
[ 255]             const { fileId, storageName, signedUrl } = await reqUrlResponse.json();
[ 256]             let reqUrlResponseObj = { fileId, storageName };
[ 257] 
[ 258]             await withRetry(() => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
[ 259]                 const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
[ 260]                 
[ 261]                 xhr.open('PUT', signedUrl);
[ 262]                 xhr.setRequestHeader('Content-Type', file.mimeType || 'application/octet-stream');
[ 263]                 
[ 264]                 xhr.upload.onprogress = (event) => {
[ 265]                     if (event.lengthComputable) {
[ 266]                         setProgress(Math.round((event.loaded / event.total) * 100));
[ 267]                     }
[ 268]                 };
[ 269]                 
[ 270]                 xhr.onload = () => {
[ 271]                     if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) resolve(true);
[ 272]                     else reject(new Error('Erreur upload R2: ' + xhr.status));
[ 273]                 };
[ 274]                 xhr.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Erreur réseau lors de l\'upload (Vérifiez votre connexion)'));
[ 275]                 
[ 276]                 if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 277]                     // On envoie le fichier brut (File object) via XHR.
[ 278]                     // XHR gère nativement le streaming depuis le disque sur Android Chrome,
[ 279]                     // évitant les crash OOM (Uffa!) depuis qu'on utilise DocumentPicker.
[ 280]                     xhr.send(file.file || file);
[ 281]                 } else {
[ 282]                     xhr.send({ uri: file.uri, type: file.mimeType, name: file.name } as any);
[ 283]                 }
[ 284]             }));
[ 285] 
[ 286]             setProgress(100);
[ 287] 
[ 288]             const confirmResponse = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/upload/confirm`, {
[ 289]                 method: 'POST',
[ 290]                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[ 291]                 body: JSON.stringify({
[ 292]                     fileId: reqUrlResponseObj.fileId,
[ 293]                     originalName: file.name,
[ 294]                     storageName: reqUrlResponseObj.storageName,
[ 295]                     userId: session?.user?.id || null
[ 296]                 })
[ 297]             });
[ 298] 
[ 299]             if (!confirmResponse.ok) {
[ 300]                 throw new Error('Erreur lors de la confirmation serveur');
[ 301]             }
[ 302] 
[ 303]             const data = await confirmResponse.json();
[ 304]             setResult(data);
[ 305] 
[ 306]             const transfer = {
[ 307]                 id: data.id,
[ 308]                 fileName: file.name,
[ 309]                 downloadUrl: data.downloadUrl,
[ 310]                 sentAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', {
[ 311]                     day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
[ 312]                     hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'
[ 313]                 }),
[ 314]                 status: 'pending',
[ 315]             };
[ 316] 
[ 317]             const existing = await AsyncStorage.getItem('faas_history');
[ 318]             const history = existing ? JSON.parse(existing) : [];
[ 319]             history.unshift(transfer);
[ 320]             await AsyncStorage.setItem('faas_history', JSON.stringify(history));
[ 321] 
[ 322]             setStep('done');
[ 323] 
[ 324]         } catch (error: any) {
[ 325]             setIsPreparing(false);
[ 326]             console.error('Upload Error:', error);
[ 327]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') window.alert(t('common.error') + '\nLe transfert a échoué: ' + error.message);
[ 328]             else Alert.alert(t('common.error'), 'Le transfert a échoué: ' + error.message);
[ 329]             setStep('category');
[ 330]         }
[ 331]     };
[ 332] 
[ 333]     const uploadFileV2 = async (file: any) => {
[ 334]         // La R&D a prouvé de manière définitive que la lecture d'un fichier > 1 Go
[ 335]         // via File.slice(), File.stream() ou FileReader provoque un "network error"
[ 336]         // (IPC bridge crash) sur Android Chrome.
[ 337]         // La seule méthode stable est le streaming natif C++ via xhr.send(File).
[ 338]         // Nous repassons donc sur l'architecture V1 (Legacy) qui est l'état de l'art mobile.
[ 339]         return uploadFileLegacy(file);
[ 340]     };
[ 341] 
[ 342]     const uploadFileMultipart = async (file: any, originalName?: string) => {
[ 343]         setProgress(0);
[ 344]         setIsPreparing(false);
[ 345]         setStep('uploading');
[ 346] 
[ 347]         const finalName = originalName || file.name || 'fichier.zip';
[ 348]         const theFile = file.file || file;
[ 349]         
[ 350]         if (typeof theFile.slice !== 'function') {
[ 351]             return uploadFileLegacy(file);
[ 352]         }
[ 353] 
[ 354]         const CHUNK_SIZE = 10 * 1024 * 1024; // 10 MB
[ 355]         const totalParts = Math.ceil(theFile.size / CHUNK_SIZE);
[ 356] 
[ 357]         try {
[ 358]             const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
[ 359]             
[ 360]             const startRes = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/upload/multipart/start`, {
[ 361]                 method: 'POST',
[ 362]                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[ 363]                 body: JSON.stringify({
[ 364]                     fileName: finalName,
[ 365]                     contentType: file.mimeType || file.type || 'application/octet-stream',
[ 366]                     totalParts,
[ 367]                     userId: session?.user?.id || null
[ 368]                 })
[ 369]             });
[ 370] 
[ 371]             if (!startRes.ok) throw new Error('Erreur start multipart');
[ 372]             const { fileId, storageName, uploadId, presignedUrls } = await startRes.json();
[ 373] 
[ 374]             const parts: { PartNumber: number, ETag: string }[] = [];
[ 375]             let uploadedBytes = 0;
[ 376] 
[ 377]             const uploadChunk = async (partNumber: number) => {
[ 378]                 const start = (partNumber - 1) * CHUNK_SIZE;
[ 379]                 const end = Math.min(start + CHUNK_SIZE, theFile.size);
[ 380]                 const chunk = theFile.slice(start, end);
[ 381]                 const url = presignedUrls[partNumber];
[ 382] 
[ 383]                 return await withRetry(async () => {
[ 384]                     return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
[ 385]                         const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
[ 386]                         xhr.open('PUT', url);
[ 387]                         xhr.onload = () => {
[ 388]                             if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
[ 389]                                 let etag = xhr.getResponseHeader('ETag');
[ 390]                                 if (etag) etag = etag.replace(/"/g, ''); 
[ 391]                                 parts.push({ PartNumber: partNumber, ETag: etag || 'mock-etag' });
[ 392]                                 uploadedBytes += chunk.size;
[ 393]                                 setProgress(Math.round((uploadedBytes / theFile.size) * 100));
[ 394]                                 resolve(true);
[ 395]                             } else {
[ 396]                                 reject(new Error('Erreur chunk ' + partNumber));
[ 397]                             }
[ 398]                         };
[ 399]                         xhr.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Erreur réseau chunk'));
[ 400]                         xhr.send(chunk);
[ 401]                     });
[ 402]                 }, 3);
[ 403]             };
[ 404] 
[ 405]             const CONCURRENCY = 4;
[ 406]             let currentPart = 1;
[ 407]             const workers = Array(Math.min(CONCURRENCY, totalParts)).fill(null).map(async () => {
[ 408]                 while (currentPart <= totalParts) {
[ 409]                     const p = currentPart++;
[ 410]                     await uploadChunk(p);
[ 411]                 }
[ 412]             });
[ 413]             await Promise.all(workers);
[ 414] 
[ 415]             parts.sort((a, b) => a.PartNumber - b.PartNumber);
[ 416]             
[ 417]             const completeRes = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/upload/multipart/complete`, {
[ 418]                 method: 'POST',
[ 419]                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[ 420]                 body: JSON.stringify({ storageName, uploadId, parts })
[ 421]             });
[ 422] 
[ 423]             if (!completeRes.ok) throw new Error('Erreur complete multipart');
[ 424] 
[ 425]             const confirmResponse = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/upload/confirm`, {
[ 426]                 method: 'POST',
[ 427]                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[ 428]                 body: JSON.stringify({
[ 429]                     fileId: fileId,
[ 430]                     originalName: finalName,
[ 431]                     storageName: storageName,
[ 432]                     userId: session?.user?.id || null
[ 433]                 })
[ 434]             });
[ 435] 
[ 436]             if (!confirmResponse.ok) throw new Error('Erreur confirmation backend');
[ 437] 
[ 438]             const data = await confirmResponse.json();
[ 439]             setResult(data);
[ 440] 
[ 441]             const transfer = {
[ 442]                 id: data.id,
[ 443]                 fileName: finalName,
[ 444]                 downloadUrl: data.downloadUrl,
[ 445]                 sentAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', {
[ 446]                     day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
[ 447]                     hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'
[ 448]                 }),
[ 449]                 status: 'pending',
[ 450]             };
[ 451] 
[ 452]             const existing = await AsyncStorage.getItem('faas_history');
[ 453]             const history = existing ? JSON.parse(existing) : [];
[ 454]             history.unshift(transfer);
[ 455]             await AsyncStorage.setItem('faas_history', JSON.stringify(history));
[ 456] 
[ 457]             setStep('done');
[ 458] 
[ 459]         } catch (error: any) {
[ 460]             setIsPreparing(false);
[ 461]             console.error('Multipart Error:', error);
[ 462]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') window.alert(t('common.error') + '\nLe transfert a échoué: ' + error.message);
[ 463]             else Alert.alert(t('common.error'), 'Le transfert a échoué: ' + error.message);
[ 464]             setStep('category');
[ 465]         }
[ 466]     };
[ 467] 
[ 468]     const uploadMultipleFiles = async (files: any[]) => {
[ 469]         setProgress(0);
[ 470]         setIsPreparing(false);
[ 471]         setStep('uploading');
[ 472] 
[ 473]         try {
[ 474]             const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
[ 475]             
[ 476]             const filePayload = files.map(f => ({
[ 477]                 fileName: f.name,
[ 478]                 contentType: f.mimeType || 'application/octet-stream'
[ 479]             }));
[ 480] 
[ 481]             const reqUrlResponse = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/upload/request-urls`, {
[ 482]                 method: 'POST',
[ 483]                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[ 484]                 body: JSON.stringify({ files: filePayload })
[ 485]             });
[ 486] 
[ 487]             if (!reqUrlResponse.ok) {
[ 488]                 throw new Error('Erreur lors de la demande de tickets au serveur');
[ 489]             }
[ 490] 
[ 491]             const { batchId, uploadTickets } = await reqUrlResponse.json();
[ 492] 
[ 493]             const progressMap = new Array(files.length).fill(0);
[ 494]             const totalFiles = files.length;
[ 495] 
[ 496]             const updateGlobalProgress = () => {
[ 497]                 const total = progressMap.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + curr, 0);
[ 498]                 setProgress(Math.round(total / totalFiles));
[ 499]             };
[ 500] 
[ 501]             const CONCURRENCY_LIMIT = 3;
[ 502]             let currentIndex = 0;
[ 503] 
[ 504]             const uploadTaskWorker = async () => {
[ 505]                 while (currentIndex < files.length) {
[ 506]                     const i = currentIndex++;
[ 507]                     const file = files[i];
[ 508]                     const ticket = uploadTickets[i];
[ 509] 
[ 510]                     await withRetry(() => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
[ 511]                         const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
[ 512]                         xhr.open('PUT', ticket.signedUrl);
[ 513]                         xhr.setRequestHeader('Content-Type', file.mimeType || 'application/octet-stream');
[ 514]                         
[ 515]                         xhr.upload.onprogress = (event) => {
[ 516]                             if (event.lengthComputable) {
[ 517]                                 progressMap[i] = (event.loaded / event.total) * 100;
[ 518]                                 updateGlobalProgress();
[ 519]                             }
[ 520]                         };
[ 521] 
[ 522]                         xhr.onload = () => {
[ 523]                             if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
[ 524]                                 progressMap[i] = 100;
[ 525]                                 updateGlobalProgress();
[ 526]                                 resolve(true);
[ 527]                             } else reject(new Error('Erreur upload R2: ' + xhr.status));
[ 528]                         };
[ 529]                         xhr.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Erreur réseau'));
[ 530] 
[ 531]                         if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 532]                             if (file.file && file.file.size > 100 * 1024 * 1024) { // > 100 MB
[ 533]                                 let fakeP = 0;
[ 534]                                 const interval = setInterval(() => {
[ 535]                                     if (fakeP < 90) { fakeP += 2; progressMap[i] = fakeP; updateGlobalProgress(); }
[ 536]                                 }, 1000);
[ 537]                                 let fetchBody = file.file;
[ 538]                                 let fetchOptions: any = {
[ 539]                                     method: 'PUT', 
[ 540]                                     body: fetchBody, 
[ 541]                                     headers: { 'Content-Type': file.mimeType || 'application/octet-stream' }
[ 542]                                 };
[ 543]                                 fetch(ticket.signedUrl, fetchOptions).then(res => {
[ 544]                                     clearInterval(interval);
[ 545]                                     if (res.ok) { progressMap[i] = 100; updateGlobalProgress(); resolve(true); }
[ 546]                                     else reject(new Error('Erreur upload R2'));
[ 547]                                 }).catch(e => { clearInterval(interval); reject(e); });
[ 548]                                 return;
[ 549]                             }
[ 550]                             xhr.send(file.file || file);
[ 551]                         } else {
[ 552]                             // React Native gère nativement l'envoi depuis une URI content://
[ 553]                             xhr.send({ uri: file.uri, type: file.mimeType, name: file.name } as any);
[ 554]                         }
[ 555]                     }));
[ 556]                 }
[ 557]             };
[ 558] 
[ 559]             const workers = Array(Math.min(CONCURRENCY_LIMIT, files.length)).fill(null).map(() => uploadTaskWorker());
[ 560]             await Promise.all(workers);
[ 561] 
[ 562]             setProgress(100);
[ 563] 
[ 564]             // 4. Confirmation finale
[ 565]             const confirmResponse = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/upload/confirm`, {
[ 566]                 method: 'POST',
[ 567]                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[ 568]                 body: JSON.stringify({
[ 569]                     fileId: batchId,
[ 570]                     files: uploadTickets.map((t: any) => ({
[ 571]                         originalName: t.originalName,
[ 572]                         storageName: t.storageName
[ 573]                     })),
[ 574]                     userId: session?.user?.id || null
[ 575]                 })
[ 576]             });
[ 577] 
[ 578]             if (!confirmResponse.ok) {
[ 579]                 throw new Error('Erreur lors de la confirmation serveur');
[ 580]             }
[ 581] 
[ 582]             const data = await confirmResponse.json();
[ 583]             
[ 584]             // Simulation d'un fichier fictif pour afficher le nom du lot sur l'écran "done"
[ 585]             setSelectedFile({ name: `Lot de ${files.length} fichiers` });
[ 586]             setResult(data);
[ 587] 
[ 588]             const transfer = {
[ 589]                 id: data.id,
[ 590]                 fileName: `Lot de ${files.length} fichiers`,
[ 591]                 downloadUrl: data.downloadUrl,
[ 592]                 sentAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', {
[ 593]                     day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
[ 594]                     hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'
[ 595]                 }),
[ 596]                 status: 'pending',
[ 597]             };
[ 598] 
[ 599]             const existing = await AsyncStorage.getItem('faas_history');
[ 600]             const history = existing ? JSON.parse(existing) : [];
[ 601]             history.unshift(transfer);
[ 602]             await AsyncStorage.setItem('faas_history', JSON.stringify(history));
[ 603] 
[ 604]             setStep('done');
[ 605] 
[ 606]         } catch (error: any) {
[ 607]             setIsPreparing(false);
[ 608]             console.error('Upload Error:', error);
[ 609]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') window.alert(t('common.error') + '\nLe transfert a échoué: ' + error.message);
[ 610]             else Alert.alert(t('common.error'), 'Le transfert a échoué: ' + error.message);
[ 611]             setStep('category');
[ 612]         }
[ 613]     };
[ 614] 
[ 615]     const shareLink = async () => {
[ 616]         try {
[ 617]             if (Platform.OS === 'web' && navigator.share) {
[ 618]                 await navigator.share({
[ 619]                     title: 'Fast Transfer',
[ 620]                     text: "Je t'ai envoyé des fichiers via Fast Transfer. Télécharge-les ici :",
[ 621]                     url: result?.downloadUrl
[ 622]                 });
[ 623]             } else {
[ 624]                 await Share.share({
[ 625]                     message: `Je t'ai envoyé des fichiers via Fast Transfer. Télécharge-les ici : ${result?.downloadUrl}`,
[ 626]                 });
[ 627]             }
[ 628]         } catch (error) {
[ 629]             console.error('Erreur de partage', error);
[ 630]         }
[ 631]     };
[ 632] 
[ 633]     const copyLink = () => {
[ 634]         Clipboard.setString(result?.downloadUrl || '');
[ 635]         setCopied(true);
[ 636]         setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 3000);
[ 637]     };
[ 638] 
[ 639]     const reset = () => {
[ 640]         setStep('category');
[ 641]         setPendingFiles([]);
[ 642]         setSelectedFile(null);
[ 643]         setProgress(0);
[ 644]         setResult(null);
[ 645]         setCopied(false);
[ 646]         setIsPreparing(false);
[ 647]     };
[ 648] 
[ 649]     if (step === 'category') {
[ 650]         return (
[ 651]             <View style={styles.container}>
[ 652]                 <View style={styles.backgroundGlow} pointerEvents="none" />
[ 653]                 
[ 654]                 <View style={styles.contentWrapper}>
[ 655]                     <Pressable 
[ 656]                         style={({ pressed, hovered }: any) => [
[ 657]                             styles.backButton,
[ 658]                             (pressed || hovered) && styles.backButtonHovered
[ 659]                         ]}
[ 660]                         onPress={() => router.push('/')}>
[ 661]                         <Ionicons name="arrow-back-outline" size={18} color={colors.textMuted} />
[ 662]                         <Text style={styles.backButtonText}>{t('common.back')}</Text>
[ 663]                     </Pressable>
[ 664]                     
[ 665]                     <Text style={styles.title}>{t('send.title')}</Text>
[ 666]                     <Text style={styles.subtitle}>{t('send.subtitle')}</Text>
[ 667]                     
[ 668]                     <View style={styles.categoriesGrid}>
[ 669]                         {CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
[ 670]                             <ActionCard
[ 671]                                 key={cat.id}
[ 672]                                 title={cat.label}
[ 673]                                 description="" 
[ 674]                                 icon={cat.icon as any}
[ 675]                                 onPress={() => pickFile(cat.id, cat.mimeTypes)}
[ 676]                                 style={styles.categoryCard} 
[ 677]                                 compact={true} 
[ 678]                             />
[ 679]                         ))}
[ 680]                     </View>
[ 681]                     
[ 682]                     {(isPreparing || isPicking) && (
[ 683]                         <View style={styles.preparingOverlay}>
[ 684]                             <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
[ 685]                             <Text style={styles.preparingText}>
[ 686]                                 {isPicking 
[ 687]                                     ? 'Traitement en cours...' 
[ 688]                                     : (batchStats ? `Préparation de ${batchStats.count} fichiers...` : 'Préparation des fichiers...')
[ 689]                                 }
[ 690]                             </Text>
[ 691]                         </View>
[ 692]                     )}
[ 693]                 </View>
[ 694]             </View>
[ 695]         );
[ 696]     }
[ 697] 
[ 698]     if (step === 'review') {
[ 699]         const totalSize = pendingFiles.reduce((acc, f) => acc + (f.size || f.fileSize || f.file?.size || 0), 0);
[ 700]         
[ 701]         const startUploadFlow = async () => {
[ 702]             if (pendingFiles.length === 0) return;
[ 703]             setIsPreparing(true);
[ 704]             
[ 705]             if (pendingFiles.length === 1) {
[ 706]                 const theFile = pendingFiles[0];
[ 707]                 setSelectedFile(theFile);
[ 708]                 setBatchStats(null);
[ 709]                 
[ 710]                 const fileObj = theFile.file || theFile;
[ 711]                 if (Platform.OS === 'web' && fileObj.size > 20 * 1024 * 1024 && typeof fileObj.slice === 'function') {
[ 712]                     await uploadFileMultipart(theFile, theFile.name);
[ 713]                 } else {
[ 714]                     await uploadFileV2(theFile);
[ 715]                 }
[ 716]             } else {
[ 717]                 setBatchStats({ count: pendingFiles.length, totalSize });
[ 718]                 setSelectedFile({ name: `Lot de ${pendingFiles.length} fichiers` });
[ 719]                 
[ 720]                 if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 721]                     try {
[ 722]                         const zip = new JSZip();
[ 723]                         pendingFiles.forEach(f => {
[ 724]                             zip.file(f.name, f.file || f);
[ 725]                         });
[ 726]                         
[ 727]                         const zipBlob: any = await zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob' }, (metadata) => {
[ 728]                             // Progression de la préparation
[ 729]                         });
[ 730]                         zipBlob.name = `projet_zippe_${Date.now()}.zip`;
[ 731]                         zipBlob.mimeType = 'application/zip';
[ 732]                         
[ 733]                         const fileObj = { file: zipBlob, name: zipBlob.name, mimeType: 'application/zip' };
[ 734]                         
[ 735]                         if (zipBlob.size > 20 * 1024 * 1024 && typeof zipBlob.slice === 'function') {
[ 736]                             await uploadFileMultipart(fileObj, zipBlob.name);
[ 737]                         } else {
[ 738]                             await uploadFileV2(fileObj);
[ 739]                         }
[ 740]                     } catch (e) {
[ 741]                         console.error("Zipping error:", e);
[ 742]                         await uploadMultipleFiles(pendingFiles);
[ 743]                     }
[ 744]                 } else {
[ 745]                     await uploadMultipleFiles(pendingFiles);
[ 746]                 }
[ 747]             }
[ 748]         };
[ 749] 
[ 750]         return (
[ 751]             <SafeAreaView style={styles.container}>
[ 752]                 <View style={styles.backgroundGlow} pointerEvents="none" />
[ 753]                 
[ 754]                 <View style={{ width: '100%', paddingHorizontal: 20, paddingTop: Platform.OS === 'web' ? 40 : 20, zIndex: 20 }}>
[ 755]                     <Pressable 
[ 756]                         style={({ pressed, hovered }: any) => [
[ 757]                             styles.backButton,
[ 758]                             (pressed || hovered) && styles.backButtonHovered
[ 759]                         ]}
[ 760]                         onPress={reset}>
[ 761]                         <Ionicons name="arrow-back-outline" size={18} color={colors.textMuted} />
[ 762]                         <Text style={styles.backButtonText}>Annuler et retour</Text>
[ 763]                     </Pressable>
[ 764]                 </View>
[ 765] 
[ 766]                 <View style={[styles.centerContent, { flex: 1 }]}>
[ 767]                     <Ionicons name="folder-open-outline" size={64} color={colors.primary} style={{ marginBottom: 20 }} />
[ 768]                     <Text style={styles.uploadingTitle}>Panier de transfert</Text>
[ 769]                     <Text style={styles.uploadingFile}>
[ 770]                         {pendingFiles.length} fichier{pendingFiles.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} prêt{pendingFiles.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}
[ 771]                     </Text>
[ 772]                     <Text style={[styles.subtitle, { marginBottom: 40 }]}>
[ 773]                         Taille totale : {formatSize(totalSize)}
[ 774]                     </Text>
[ 775] 
[ 776]                     <Pressable 
[ 777]                         style={({ pressed, hovered }: any) => [
[ 778]                             styles.primaryButton,
[ 779]                             (pressed || hovered) && styles.primaryButtonHovered,
[ 780]                             { width: '100%', marginBottom: 15 },
[ 781]                             isPicking && { opacity: 0.5 }
[ 782]                         ]} 
[ 783]                         disabled={isPicking}
[ 784]                         onPress={startUploadFlow}>
[ 785]                         <Text style={styles.primaryButtonText}>Envoyer ({pendingFiles.length})</Text>
[ 786]                     </Pressable>
[ 787] 
[ 788]                     <Pressable 
[ 789]                         style={({ pressed, hovered }: any) => [
[ 790]                             styles.secondaryButton,
[ 791]                             (pressed || hovered) && styles.secondaryButtonHovered,
[ 792]                             { width: '100%' },
[ 793]                             isPicking && { opacity: 0.5 }
[ 794]                         ]} 
[ 795]                         disabled={isPicking}
[ 796]                         onPress={() => pickFile(lastCategory, lastMimeTypes)}>
[ 797]                         <Text style={styles.secondaryButtonText}>
[ 798]                             {isPicking ? (
[ 799]                                 <>
[ 800]                                     <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={colors.text} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
[ 801]                                     Traitement...
[ 802]                                 </>
[ 803]                             ) : (
[ 804]                                 <>
[ 805]                                     <Ionicons name="add-circle-outline" size={18} /> Ajouter d'autres fichiers
[ 806]                                 </>
[ 807]                             )}
[ 808]                         </Text>
[ 809]                     </Pressable>
[ 810]                     
[ 811]                     <Pressable 
[ 812]                         style={{ marginTop: 20, padding: 10 }}
[ 813]                         onPress={reset}>
[ 814]                         <Text style={[styles.secondaryButtonText, { color: colors.error }]}>Vider le panier</Text>
[ 815]                     </Pressable>
[ 816]                 </View>
[ 817]                 
[ 818]                 {isPicking && (
[ 819]                     <View style={styles.fullscreenOverlay}>
[ 820]                         <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
[ 821]                         <Text style={styles.overlayText}>Traitement en cours...</Text>
[ 822]                         <Text style={styles.overlaySubText}>Veuillez patienter, cela peut prendre quelques secondes.</Text>
[ 823]                     </View>
[ 824]                 )}
[ 825]             </SafeAreaView>
[ 826]         );
[ 827]     }
[ 828] 
[ 829]     if (step === 'uploading') {
[ 830]         const radius = 60;
[ 831]         const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
[ 832]         const strokeDashoffset = circumference - (progress / 100) * circumference;
[ 833] 
[ 834]         return (
[ 835]             <SafeAreaView style={styles.container}>
[ 836]                 <View style={styles.backgroundGlow} pointerEvents="none" />
[ 837]                 <View style={styles.centerContent}>
[ 838]

[ 840]                     <Text style={styles.uploadingFile}>
[ 841]                         {batchStats ? `Lot de ${batchStats.count} fichiers (${formatSize(batchStats.totalSize)})` : selectedFile?.name}
[ 842]                     </Text>
[ 843] 
[ 844]                     <View style={{ position: 'relative', width: 140, height: 140, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', marginVertical: 30 }}>
[ 845]                         <Svg width="140" height="140" viewBox="0 0 140 140">
[ 846]                             <Circle 
[ 847]                                 cx="70" cy="70" r={radius} 
[ 848]                                 stroke={colors.border} 
[ 849]                                 strokeWidth="8" fill="transparent" 
[ 850]                             />
[ 851]                             <Circle 
[ 852]                                 cx="70" cy="70" r={radius} 
[ 853]                                 stroke={colors.primary} 
[ 854]                                 strokeWidth="8" fill="transparent" 
[ 855]                                 strokeDasharray={circumference}
[ 856]                                 strokeDashoffset={strokeDashoffset}

[ 860]                             />

[  64]     const saveSettings = async (key: string, value: string) => {

[ 143]                                     {theme === tItem.id ? (
[ 144]                                         <Ionicons name="radio-button-on" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
[ 145]                                     ) : (
[ 146]                                         <Ionicons name="radio-button-off" size={20} color={colors.textSubtle} />
[ 147]                                     )}
[ 148]                                 </Pressable>
[ 149]                             ))}
[ 150]                         </View>
[ 151]

[ 157]                                     key={lang.id}
[ 158]                                     style={({ pressed, hovered }: any) => [
[ 159]                                         styles.optionCard,
[ 160]                                         language === lang.id && styles.optionCardActive,
[ 161]                                         (pressed || hovered) && styles.optionCardHovered,
[ 162]                                     ]}
[ 163]                                     onPress={() => handleLanguageChange(lang.id)}>
[ 164]                                     <Text style={[
[ 165]                                         styles.optionLabel,
[ 166]                                         language === lang.id && styles.optionLabelActive,
[ 167]                                     ]}>
[ 168]                                         {lang.label}
[ 169]                                     </Text>
[ 170]                                     {language === lang.id ? (
[ 171]                                         <Ionicons name="radio-button-on" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
[ 172]                                     ) : (

[ 174]                                     )}
[ 175]                                 </Pressable>

[ 177]                         </View>

[  46] // Helper pour charger dynamiquement PDF.js sur le Web
[  47] const loadPdfJs = (): Promise<any> => {
[  48]     return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
[  49]         if ((window as any).pdfjsLib) {
[  50]             resolve((window as any).pdfjsLib);
[  51]             return;
[  52]         }
[  53]         const script = document.createElement('script');
[  54]         script.src = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.min.js';
[  55]         script.onload = () => {
[  56]             const pdfjsLib = (window as any).pdfjsLib;
[  57]             pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
[  58]             resolve(pdfjsLib);
[  59]         };
[  60]         script.onerror = () => reject(new Error("Failed to load pdf.js"));
[  61]         document.body.appendChild(script);
[  62]     });
[  63] };
[  64]

[ 124] export default function ConvertScreen() {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Déclaration du composant UI principal de ce fichier.
[ 125]     const router = useRouter();
[ 126]     const { colors } = useTheme();
[ 127]     const { t } = useTranslation();
[ 128]     const styles = getStyles(colors);
[ 129] 
[ 130]     // Ajout de l'état "tool_intro"
[ 131]     const [step, setStep] = useState<'menu' | 'tool_intro' | 'staging' | 'split_editor' | 'sign_choice' | 'request_signature' | 'signature_request_done' | 'pdf_editor' | 'watermark_editor' | 'rotation_editor' | 'organize_editor' | 'merge_editor' | 'preparing_editor' | 'processing' | 'done'>('menu');
[ 132]     const [localError, setLocalError] = useState<string | null>(null);
[ 133]     const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'files' | 'media'>('files');

[ 155]     const [pdfOriginalBuffer, setPdfOriginalBuffer] = useState<ArrayBuffer | null>(null);

[ 157]     const [conversionQuality, setConversionQuality] = useState<ConversionQuality>('standard');
[ 158]     const [numberingConfig, setNumberingConfig] = useState<NumberingConfig>({ position: 'bottom-center', format: 'total' });
[ 159]     const [ocrLang, setOcrLang] = useState<OcrLanguage>('fra');
[ 160] 
[ 161]     // E-signature states
[ 162]     const [signers, setSigners] = useState<{name: string, email: string}[]>([{ name: '', email: '' }]);
[ 163]     const [isSendingRequest, setIsSendingRequest] = useState(false);
