[ 188]     };
[ 189] 
[ 190]     // initSplitPDF removed in favor of initOrganizeEditor
[ 191] 
[ 192]     const initPdfEditor = async (file: any, targetStep: string = 'pdf_editor') => {
[ 193]         setStep('preparing_editor');
[ 194]         try {
[ 195]             // 1. Lire le buffer original
[ 196]             let arrayBuffer: ArrayBuffer;
[ 197]             if (Platform.OS === 'web' && file.file) {
[ 198]                 arrayBuffer = await file.file.arrayBuffer();
[ 199]             } else {
[ 200]                 const response = await fetch(file.uri);
[ 201]                 arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
[ 202]             }
[ 203]             setPdfOriginalBuffer(arrayBuffer);
[ 204] 
[ 205]             let pages: string[] = [];
[ 206] 
[ 207]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 208]                 try {
[ 209]                     const pdfjsLib = await loadPdfJs();
[ 210]                     const loadingTask = pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: arrayBuffer.slice(0) });
[ 211]                     const pdf = await loadingTask.promise;
[ 212]                     
[ 213]                     // Only need first page for protect and compress, need all for watermark/rotate
[ 214]                     const maxPages = (targetStep === 'protect_editor' || targetStep === 'compress_editor') ? 1 : Math.min(pdf.numPages, 50); // Cap at 50 to prevent freezing for watermark
[ 215]                     
[ 216]                     for (let j = 1; j <= maxPages; j++) {
[ 217]                         const page = await pdf.getPage(j);
[ 218]                         const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.0 });
[ 219]                         const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
[ 220]                         const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
[ 221]                         if (ctx) {
[ 222]                             canvas.width = viewport.width;
[ 223]                             canvas.height = viewport.height;
[ 224]                             await page.render({ canvasContext: ctx, viewport }).promise;
[ 225]                             pages.push(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8));
[ 226]                         }
[ 227]                     }
[ 228]                 } catch (e) {
[ 229]                     console.warn("Local PDF rendering failed, falling back to backend", e);
[ 230]                 }
[ 231]             }
[ 232]             
[ 233]             // Si Web a échoué ou si on est sur Mobile, on utilise le backend
[ 234]             if (pages.length === 0) {
[ 235]                 const formData = new FormData();
[ 236]                 let fileBlob;
[ 237]                 if (Platform.OS === 'web' && file.file) {
[ 238]                     fileBlob = file.file;
[ 239]                 } else {
[ 240]                     const response_file = await fetch(file.uri);
[ 241]                     fileBlob = await response_file.blob();
[ 242]                 }
[ 243]                 formData.append('file', fileBlob, file.name);
[ 244] 
[ 245]                 const res = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/convert/pdf-to-image?format=jpeg&quality=standard`, {
[ 246]                     method: 'POST',
[ 247]                     body: formData,
[ 248]                     headers: { 'Accept': 'application/zip, image/jpeg' }
[ 249]                 });
[ 250] 
[ 251]                 if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Échec de la génération des images: " + await res.text());
[ 252] 
[ 253]                 const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type');
[ 254]                 const blob = await res.blob();
[ 255] 
[ 256]                 if (contentType?.includes('zip')) {
[ 257]                     const zip = new JSZip();
[ 258]                     const unzipped = await zip.loadAsync(blob);
[ 259]                     const fileNames = Object.keys(unzipped.files).sort(); 
[ 260]                     // Limiter aussi pour le backend si ce n'est que protect/compress
[ 261]                     const limit = (targetStep === 'protect_editor' || targetStep === 'compress_editor') ? 1 : fileNames.length;
[ 262]                     for (let j = 0; j < Math.min(limit, fileNames.length); j++) {
[ 263]                         const filename = fileNames[j];
[ 264]                         const f = unzipped.files[filename];
[ 265]                         if (!f.dir) {
[ 266]                             const imgBlob = await f.async('blob');
[ 267]                             pages.push(URL.createObjectURL(imgBlob));
[ 268]                         }
[ 269]                     }
[ 270]                 } else {
[ 271]                     pages.push(URL.createObjectURL(blob));
[ 272]                 }
[ 273]             }
[ 274] 
[ 275]             setPdfEditorPages(pages);
[ 276]             setStep(targetStep as any);
[ 277]         } catch (e: any) {
[ 278]             console.error("Error in initPdfEditor:", e);
[ 279]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 280]                 window.alert("Erreur: Impossible de préparer l'éditeur PDF. Détails: " + (e.message || String(e)));
[ 281]             } else {
[ 282]                 Alert.alert("Erreur", "Impossible de préparer l'éditeur PDF. Veuillez réessayer.");
[ 283]             }
[ 284]             setStep('staging');
[ 285]         }
[ 286]     };
[ 287] 
[ 288]     const handlePdfEditorComplete = async (edits: PdfEditItem[]) => {
[ 289]         if (!pdfOriginalBuffer) return;
[ 290]         setStep('processing');
[ 291]         try {
[ 292]             const pdfDoc = await PDFDocument.load(pdfOriginalBuffer);
[ 293]             const pdfPages = pdfDoc.getPages();
[ 294] 
[ 295]             // Embed fonts
[ 296]             const fontNormal = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.Helvetica);
[ 297]             const fontBold = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaBold);
[ 298]             const fontItalic = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaOblique);
[ 299]             const fontBoldItalic = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaBoldOblique);
[ 300] 
[ 301]             for (const edit of edits) {
[ 302]                 const page = pdfPages[edit.pageIndex];
[ 303]                 if (!page) continue;
[ 304] 
[ 305]                 const { width, height } = page.getSize();
[ 306]                 
[ 307]                 if (edit.type === 'text' && edit.text) {
[ 308]                     const x = (edit.x / 100) * width;
[ 309]                     const fontSize = edit.size || 24;
[ 310]                     const y = height - ((edit.y / 100) * height) - fontSize;
[ 311] 
[ 312]                     if (edit.backgroundColor && edit.backgroundColor.startsWith('#')) {
[ 313]                         const bgHex = edit.backgroundColor.replace('#', '');
[ 314]                         const bgR = parseInt(bgHex.substring(0, 2), 16) / 255;
[ 315]                         const bgG = parseInt(bgHex.substring(2, 4), 16) / 255;
[ 316]                         const bgB = parseInt(bgHex.substring(4, 6), 16) / 255;
[ 317]                         
[ 318]                         const rectWidth = (edit.width || 15) * (width / 100);
[ 319]                         const rectHeight = (edit.height || 4) * (height / 100);
[ 320]                         const rectY = height - ((edit.y / 100) * height) - rectHeight;
[ 321] 
[ 322]                         page.drawRectangle({
[ 323]                             x,
[ 324]                             y: rectY,
[ 325]                             width: rectWidth,
[ 326]                             height: rectHeight,
[ 327]                             color: rgb(bgR, bgG, bgB),
[ 328]                         });
[ 329]                     }
[ 330] 
[ 331]                     let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
[ 332]                     if (edit.color && edit.color.startsWith('#')) {
[ 333]                         const hex = edit.color.replace('#', '');
[ 334]                         r = parseInt(hex.substring(0, 2), 16) / 255;
[ 335]                         g = parseInt(hex.substring(2, 4), 16) / 255;
[ 336]                         b = parseInt(hex.substring(4, 6), 16) / 255;
[ 337]                     }
[ 338] 
[ 339]                     let fontToUse = fontNormal;
[ 340]                     if (edit.fontWeight === 'bold' && edit.fontStyle === 'italic') {
[ 341]                         fontToUse = fontBoldItalic;
[ 342]                     } else if (edit.fontWeight === 'bold') {
[ 343]                         fontToUse = fontBold;
[ 344]                     } else if (edit.fontStyle === 'italic') {
[ 345]                         fontToUse = fontItalic;
[ 346]                     }
[ 347] 
[ 348]                     let xOffset = 0;
[ 349]                     if (edit.textAlign === 'center' || edit.textAlign === 'right') {
[ 350]                         const textWidth = fontToUse.widthOfTextAtSize(edit.text, fontSize);
[ 351]                         if (edit.textAlign === 'center') xOffset = -textWidth / 2;
[ 352]                         if (edit.textAlign === 'right') xOffset = -textWidth;
[ 353]                     }
[ 354] 
[ 355]                     page.drawText(edit.text, {
[ 356]                         x: x + xOffset,
[ 357]                         y,
[ 358]                         size: fontSize,
[ 359]                         font: fontToUse,
[ 360]                         color: rgb(r, g, b),
[ 361]                     });
[ 362]                 }
[ 363] 
[ 364]                 if (edit.type === 'signature' && edit.signatureData) {
[ 365]                     const sig = edit.signatureData as any;
[ 366]                     const x = (edit.x / 100) * width;
[ 367]                     const rectWidth = (edit.width || 20) * (width / 100);
[ 368]                     const rectHeight = (edit.height || 10) * (height / 100);
[ 369]                     const y = height - ((edit.y / 100) * height) - rectHeight;
[ 370] 
[ 371]                     if (sig.type === 'text') {
[ 372]                         page.drawText(sig.data, {
[ 373]                             x, y, size: rectHeight * 0.8, font: fontItalic, color: rgb(0,0,0)
[ 374]                         });
[ 375]                     } else if (sig.type === 'path') {
[ 376]                         const scaleX = rectWidth / (sig.width || 500);
[ 377]                         const scaleY = rectHeight / (sig.height || 200);
[ 378]                         const scale = Math.min(scaleX, scaleY);
[ 379]                         page.drawSvgPath(sig.data, {
[ 380]                             x, y: y + rectHeight, scale, borderColor: rgb(0,0,0), borderWidth: 2
[ 381]                         });
[ 382]                     } else if (sig.type === 'image') {
[ 383]                         try {
[ 384]                             const isPng = sig.data.includes('image/png') || sig.data.endsWith('.png');
[ 385]                             // Si c'est une URI locale (ImagePicker), on charge via fetch
[ 386]                             const imgBytes = await fetch(sig.data).then(r => r.arrayBuffer());
[ 387]                             let pdfImg;
[ 388]                             if (isPng) {
[ 389]                                 pdfImg = await pdfDoc.embedPng(imgBytes);
[ 390]                             } else {
[ 391]                                 pdfImg = await pdfDoc.embedJpg(imgBytes);
[ 392]                             }
[ 393]                             page.drawImage(pdfImg, {
[ 394]                                 x, y, width: rectWidth, height: rectHeight
[ 395]                             });
[ 396]                         } catch (e) {
[ 397]                             console.error("Failed to embed signature image", e);
[ 398]                         }
[ 399]                     }
[ 400]                 }
[ 401]             }
[ 402] 
[ 403]             const modifiedPdfBytes = await pdfDoc.save();
[ 404]             const blob = new Blob([modifiedPdfBytes], { type: 'application/pdf' });
[ 405]             const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
[ 406]             setResultUrl(url);
[ 407]             setStep('done');
[ 408]         } catch (e: any) {
[ 409]             console.error("Error applying PDF edits:", e);
[ 410]             Alert.alert("Erreur", "Échec de l'application des modifications.");
[ 411]             setStep('staging');
[ 412]         }
[ 413]     };
[ 414] 
[ 415]     const initOrganizeEditor = async (files: any[], appendToExisting = false, targetStep: string = 'organize_editor') => {
[ 416]         try {
[ 417]             const pastelColors = ['#ffcfcf', '#cbf2e8', '#fff0b5', '#dfd5f6', '#c2ebf9'];
[ 418]             const newOrganizePages: OrganizePageItem[] = appendToExisting ? [...organizePages] : [];
[ 419]             const newOrganizeFiles: any[] = appendToExisting ? [...organizeFiles] : [];
[ 420]             const startIndex = newOrganizeFiles.length;
[ 421] 
[ 422]             if (!appendToExisting) {
[ 423]                 currentRenderSession.current += 1;
[ 424]             }
[ 425]             const sessionId = currentRenderSession.current;
[ 426]             
[ 427]             // 1. PUSH SKELETON UI IMMEDIATELY
[ 428]             for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
[ 429]                 const fileIndex = startIndex + i;
[ 430]                 const file = files[i];
[ 431]                 const color = pastelColors[fileIndex % pastelColors.length];
[ 432]                 
[ 433]                 // Add empty file with null buffer
[ 434]                 newOrganizeFiles.push({ name: file.name, color, originalIndex: fileIndex, buffer: null, pageCount: undefined });
[ 435] 
[ 436]                 // Add skeleton page to trigger ActivityIndicator instantly
[ 437]                 newOrganizePages.push({
[ 438]                     id: `${fileIndex}-0-${Date.now()}`,
[ 439]                     fileIndex: fileIndex,
[ 440]                     fileName: file.name,
[ 441]                     pageIndex: 0,
[ 442]                     imageUri: null as any
[ 443]                 });
[ 444]             }
[ 445] 
[ 446]             setOrganizeFiles(newOrganizeFiles);
[ 447]             setOrganizePages(newOrganizePages);
[ 448]             if (!appendToExisting) setStep(targetStep as any);
[ 449] 
[ 450]             // 2. PROCESS ASYNC IN BACKGROUND
[ 451]             files.forEach((file, i) => {
[ 452]                 const fileIndex = startIndex + i;
[ 453]                 
[ 454]                 setTimeout(async () => {
[ 455]                     try {
[ 456]                         let arrayBuffer: ArrayBuffer;
[ 457]                         if (Platform.OS === 'web' && file.file) {
[ 458]                             arrayBuffer = await file.file.arrayBuffer();
[ 459]                         } else {
[ 460]                             const response = await fetch(file.uri);
[ 461]                             arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
[ 462]                         }
[ 463]                         
[ 464]                         // Update buffer in state
[ 465]                         setOrganizeFiles(prev => {
[ 466]                             const next = [...prev];
[ 467]                             const target = next.find(f => f.originalIndex === fileIndex);
[ 468]                             if (target) target.buffer = arrayBuffer;
[ 469]                             return next;
[ 470]                         });
[ 471] 
[ 472]                         if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 473]                             try {
[ 474]                                 const pdfjsLib = await loadPdfJs();
[ 475]                                 const loadingTask = pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: arrayBuffer.slice(0) });
[ 476]                                 const pdf = await loadingTask.promise;
[ 477]                                 
[ 478]                                 setOrganizeFiles(prev => {
[ 479]                                     const next = [...prev];
[ 480]                                     const target = next.find(f => f.originalIndex === fileIndex);
[ 481]                                     if (target) target.pageCount = pdf.numPages;
[ 482]                                     return next;
[ 483]                                 });
[ 484]                                 
[ 485]                                 if (pdf.numPages >= 1) {
[ 486]                                     if (sessionId !== currentRenderSession.current) return;
[ 487]                                     
[ 488]                                     setOrganizePages(prev => {
[ 489]                                         const next = [...prev];
[ 490]                                         const newSkeletons = [];
[ 491]                                         for (let j = 0; j < pdf.numPages; j++) {
[ 492]                                             newSkeletons.push({
[ 493]                                                 id: `${fileIndex}-${j}-${Date.now()}`,
[ 494]                                                 fileIndex: fileIndex,
[ 495]                                                 fileName: file.name,
[ 496]                                                 pageIndex: j,
[ 497]                                                 imageUri: null as any
[ 498]                                             });
[ 499]                                         }
[ 500]                                         const targetIndex = next.findIndex(p => p.fileIndex === fileIndex);
[ 501]                                         if (targetIndex !== -1) {
[ 502]                                             let count = 0;
[ 503]                                             while (next[targetIndex + count]?.fileIndex === fileIndex) count++;
[ 504]                                             next.splice(targetIndex, count, ...newSkeletons);
[ 505]                                         } else {
[ 506]                                             next.push(...newSkeletons);
[ 507]                                         }
[ 508]                                         return next;
[ 509]                                     });
[ 510] 
[ 511]                                     const page = await pdf.getPage(1);
[ 512]                                     const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.0 });
[ 513]                                     const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
[ 514]                                     const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
[ 515]                                     canvas.width = viewport.width;
[ 516]                                     canvas.height = viewport.height;
[ 517]                                     if (ctx) {
[ 518]                                         await page.render({ canvasContext: ctx, viewport }).promise;
[ 519]                                         const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);
[ 520]                                         
[ 521]                                         if (sessionId === currentRenderSession.current) {
[ 522]                                             setOrganizePages(prev => {
[ 523]                                                 const next = [...prev];
[ 524]                                                 const target = next.find(p => p.fileIndex === fileIndex && p.pageIndex === 0);
[ 525]                                                 if (target) target.imageUri = dataUrl;
[ 526]                                                 return next;
[ 527]                                             });
[ 528]                                         }
[ 529]                                     }
[ 530]                                 }
[ 531] 
[ 532]                                 if (pdf.numPages > 1) {
[ 533]                                     for (let j = 2; j <= pdf.numPages; j++) {
[ 534]                                         if (sessionId !== currentRenderSession.current) break;
[ 535]                                         try {
[ 536]                                             const page = await pdf.getPage(j);
[ 537]                                             const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.0 });
[ 538]                                             const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
[ 539]                                             const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
[ 540]                                             canvas.width = viewport.width;
[ 541]                                             canvas.height = viewport.height;
[ 542]                                             if (ctx) {
[ 543]                                                 await page.render({ canvasContext: ctx, viewport }).promise;
[ 544]                                                 const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);
[ 545]                                                 
[ 546]                                                 if (sessionId === currentRenderSession.current) {
[ 547]                                                     setOrganizePages(prev => {
[ 548]                                                         const next = [...prev];
[ 549]                                                         const target = next.find(p => p.fileIndex === fileIndex && p.pageIndex === j - 1);
[ 550]                                                         if (target) target.imageUri = dataUrl;
[ 551]                                                         return next;
[ 552]                                                     });
[ 553]                                                 }
[ 554]                                             }
[ 555]                                         } catch (e) {
[ 556]                                             console.warn('Async thumbnail error', e);
[ 557]                                         }
[ 558]                                     }
[ 559]                                 }
[ 560]                             } catch (localError) {
[ 561]                                 console.warn("Local PDF.js rendering failed", localError);
[ 562]                             }
[ 563]                         } else {
[ 564]                             // Fallback backend for Mobile
[ 565]                             try {
[ 566]                                 const tempDoc = await PDFDocument.load(arrayBuffer.slice(0), { ignoreEncryption: true });
[ 567]                                 const numPages = tempDoc.getPageCount();
[ 568]                                 
[ 569]                                 setOrganizePages(prev => {
[ 570]                                     const next = [...prev];
[ 571]                                     const newSkeletons = [];
[ 572]                                     for (let j = 0; j < numPages; j++) {
[ 573]                                         newSkeletons.push({
[ 574]                                             id: `${fileIndex}-${j}-${Date.now()}`,
[ 575]                                             fileIndex: fileIndex,
[ 576]                                             fileName: file.name,
[ 577]                                             pageIndex: j,
[ 578]                                             imageUri: null as any
[ 579]                                         });
[ 580]                                     }
[ 581]                                     const targetIndex = next.findIndex(p => p.fileIndex === fileIndex);
[ 582]                                     if (targetIndex !== -1) {
[ 583]                                         let count = 0;
[ 584]                                         while (next[targetIndex + count]?.fileIndex === fileIndex) count++;
[ 585]                                         next.splice(targetIndex, count, ...newSkeletons);
[ 586]                                     } else {
[ 587]                                         next.push(...newSkeletons);
[ 588]                                     }
[ 589]                                     return next;
[ 590]                                 });
[ 591]                             } catch (e) {
[ 592]                                 console.warn("Failed to read page count locally for mobile", e);
[ 593]                             }
[ 594] 
[ 595]                             const formData = new FormData();
[ 596]                             let fileBlob;
[ 597]                             if (Platform.OS === 'web' && file.file) {
[ 598]                                 fileBlob = file.file;
[ 599]                             } else {
[ 600]                                 const response_file = await fetch(file.uri);
[ 601]                                 fileBlob = await response_file.blob();
[ 602]                             }
[ 603]                             formData.append('file', fileBlob, file.name);
[ 604] 
[ 605]                             const res = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/convert/pdf-to-image?format=jpeg&quality=standard`, {
[ 606]                                 method: 'POST',
[ 607]                                 body: formData,
[ 608]                                 headers: { 'Accept': 'application/zip, image/jpeg' }
[ 609]                             });
[ 610] 
[ 611]                             if (!res.ok) {
[ 612]                                 throw new Error(`Erreur backend: ${res.status}`);
[ 613]                             }
[ 614] 
[ 615]                             const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type');
[ 616]                             const blob = await res.blob();
[ 617] 
[ 618]                             if (contentType?.includes('zip')) {
[ 619]                                 const zip = new JSZip();
[ 620]                                 const unzipped = await zip.loadAsync(blob);
[ 621]                                 const fileNames = Object.keys(unzipped.files).sort();
[ 622]                                 for (let j = 0; j < fileNames.length; j++) {
[ 623]                                     const filename = fileNames[j];
[ 624]                                     const f = unzipped.files[filename];
[ 625]                                     if (!f.dir) {
[ 626]                                         const imgBlob = await f.async('blob');
[ 627]                                         if (sessionId === currentRenderSession.current) {
[ 628]                                             setOrganizePages(prev => {
[ 629]                                                 const next = [...prev];
[ 630]                                                 const target = next.find(p => p.fileIndex === fileIndex && p.pageIndex === j);
[ 631]                                                 if (target) {
[ 632]                                                     target.imageUri = URL.createObjectURL(imgBlob);
[ 633]                                                 } else {
[ 634]                                                     next.push({
[ 635]                                                         id: `${fileIndex}-${j}-${Date.now()}`,
[ 636]                                                         fileIndex: fileIndex,
[ 637]                                                         fileName: file.name,
[ 638]                                                         pageIndex: j,
[ 639]                                                         imageUri: URL.createObjectURL(imgBlob)
[ 640]                                                     });
[ 641]                                                 }
[ 642]                                                 return next;
[ 643]                                             });
[ 644]                                         }
[ 645]                                     }
[ 646]                                 }
[ 647]                             } else {
[ 648]                                 if (sessionId === currentRenderSession.current) {
[ 649]                                     setOrganizePages(prev => {
[ 650]                                         const next = [...prev];
[ 651]                                         const target = next.find(p => p.fileIndex === fileIndex && p.pageIndex === 0);
[ 652]                                         if (target) target.imageUri = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
[ 653]                                         return next;
[ 654]                                     });
[ 655]                                 }
[ 656]                             }
[ 657]                         }
[ 658]                     } catch (fileError: any) {
[ 659]                         console.error(`Erreur pour le fichier ${file.name}:`, fileError);
[ 660]                     }
[ 661]                 }, 50 * i);
[ 662]             });
[ 663]         } catch (e: any) {
[ 664]             console.error("Error in initOrganizeEditor:", e);
[ 665]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 666]                 window.alert("Erreur: Impossible de préparer l'éditeur PDF. Détails: " + (e.message || String(e)));
[ 667]             }
[ 668]             if (!appendToExisting) setStep('staging');
[ 669]         }
[ 670]     };
[ 671] 
[ 672]     const handleWatermarkComplete = async (settings: WatermarkSettings) => {
[ 673]         if (!pdfOriginalBuffer) return;
[ 674]         setStep('processing');
[ 675]         try {
[ 676]             const pdfDoc = await PDFDocument.load(pdfOriginalBuffer);
[ 677]             const pdfPages = pdfDoc.getPages();
[ 678]             const fontBold = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaBold);
[ 679] 
[ 680]             let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
[ 681]             if (settings.color && settings.color.startsWith('#')) {
[ 682]                 const hex = settings.color.replace('#', '');
[ 683]                 r = parseInt(hex.substring(0, 2), 16) / 255;
[ 684]                 g = parseInt(hex.substring(2, 4), 16) / 255;
[ 685]                 b = parseInt(hex.substring(4, 6), 16) / 255;
[ 686]             }
[ 687] 
[ 688]             // Let's improve the text centering using widthOfTextAtSize
[ 689]             pdfPages.forEach(page => {
[ 690]                 const { width, height } = page.getSize();
[ 691]                 const textWidth = fontBold.widthOfTextAtSize(settings.text, settings.size);
[ 692]                 const textHeight = settings.size; // approximate
[ 693]                 
[ 694]                 page.drawText(settings.text, {
[ 695]                     x: (width / 2) - (textWidth / 2) * Math.cos(45 * Math.PI / 180) + (textHeight/2) * Math.sin(45 * Math.PI / 180),
[ 696]                     y: (height / 2) - (textWidth / 2) * Math.sin(45 * Math.PI / 180) - (textHeight/2) * Math.cos(45 * Math.PI / 180),
[ 697]                     size: settings.size,
[ 698]                     font: fontBold,
[ 699]                     color: rgb(r, g, b),
[ 700]                     opacity: settings.opacity,
[ 701]                     rotate: degrees(45), // use positive 45 for diagonal going up-right
[ 702]                 });
[ 703]             });
[ 704] 
[ 705]             const modifiedPdfBytes = await pdfDoc.save();
[ 706]             const blob = new Blob([modifiedPdfBytes], { type: 'application/pdf' });
[ 707]             const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
[ 708]             setResultUrl(url);
[ 709]             setStep('done');
[ 710]         } catch (e: any) {
[ 711]             console.error("Error applying watermark:", e);
[ 712]             Alert.alert("Erreur", "Échec de l'application du filigrane.");
[ 713]             setStep('staging');
[ 714]         }
[ 715]     };
[ 716] 
[ 717]     const handleRotationComplete = async (rotations: number[]) => {
[ 718]         if (!pdfOriginalBuffer) return;
[ 719]         setStep('processing');
[ 720]         try {
[ 721]             const pdfDoc = await PDFDocument.load(pdfOriginalBuffer);
[ 722]             const pdfPages = pdfDoc.getPages();
[ 723]             
[ 724]             pdfPages.forEach((page, index) => {
[ 725]                 if (rotations[index] !== 0) {
[ 726]                     const currentAngle = page.getRotation().angle;
[ 727]                     page.setRotation(degrees(currentAngle + rotations[index]));
[ 728]                 }
[ 729]             });
[ 730] 
[ 731]             const modifiedPdfBytes = await pdfDoc.save();
[ 732]             const blob = new Blob([modifiedPdfBytes], { type: 'application/pdf' });
[ 733]             const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
[ 734]             setResultUrl(url);
[ 735]             setStep('done');
[ 736]         } catch (e: any) {
[ 737]             console.error("Error applying rotation:", e);
[ 738]             Alert.alert("Erreur", "Échec de l'application de la rotation.");
[ 739]             setStep('staging');
[ 740]         }
[ 741]     };
[ 742] 
[ 743]     const handleOrganizeComplete = async (orderedPages: OrganizePageItem[]) => {
[ 744]         setStep('processing');
[ 745]         setTimeout(async () => {
[ 746]             try {
[ 747]                 // Create a new empty document
[ 748]                 const newPdfDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
[ 749]                 
[ 750]                 // Pre-load all required source documents ONCE
[ 751]                 const sourceDocs = new Map<number, PDFDocument>();
[ 752]                 const uniqueFileIndices = [...new Set(orderedPages.map(p => p.fileIndex))];
[ 753]                 for (const fileIndex of uniqueFileIndices) {
[ 754]                     const sourceFileInfo = organizeFiles.find(f => f.originalIndex === fileIndex);
[ 755]                     let buffer = sourceFileInfo?.buffer;
[ 756]                     if (!buffer) {
[ 757]                         const originalFile = selectedFiles.find((_, i) => i === fileIndex);
[ 758]                         if (originalFile) {
[ 759]                             if (Platform.OS === 'web' && originalFile.file) {
[ 760]                                 buffer = await originalFile.file.arrayBuffer();
[ 761]                             } else {
[ 762]                                 const response = await fetch(originalFile.uri);
[ 763]                                 buffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
[ 764]                             }
[ 765]                         }
[ 766]                     }
[ 767]                     if (buffer) {
[ 768]                         const doc = await PDFDocument.load(buffer, { ignoreEncryption: true });
[ 769]                         sourceDocs.set(fileIndex, doc);
[ 770]                     }
[ 771]                 }
[ 772] 
[ 773]                 // Group pages to copy by fileIndex sequentially to minimize copyPages calls
[ 774]                 const fileGroups: { fileIndex: number, pageIndices: number[] }[] = [];
[ 775]                 for (const pageItem of orderedPages) {
[ 776]                     const lastGroup = fileGroups[fileGroups.length - 1];
[ 777]                     if (lastGroup && lastGroup.fileIndex === pageItem.fileIndex) {
[ 778]                         lastGroup.pageIndices.push(pageItem.pageIndex);
[ 779]                     } else {
[ 780]                         fileGroups.push({ fileIndex: pageItem.fileIndex, pageIndices: [pageItem.pageIndex] });
[ 781]                     }
[ 782]                 }
[ 783] 
[ 784]                 // Execute the bulk copies
[ 785]                 for (const group of fileGroups) {
[ 786]                     const sourceDoc = sourceDocs.get(group.fileIndex);
[ 787]                     if (sourceDoc) {
[ 788]                         const copiedPages = await newPdfDoc.copyPages(sourceDoc, group.pageIndices);
[ 789]                         for (const copiedPage of copiedPages) {
[ 790]                             newPdfDoc.addPage(copiedPage);
[ 791]                         }
[ 792]                     }
[ 793]                     await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 10)); // Yield thread
[ 794]                 }
[ 795] 
[ 796]                 const finalPdfBytes = await newPdfDoc.save();
[ 797]                 const blob = new Blob([finalPdfBytes], { type: 'application/pdf' });
[ 798]                 const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
[ 799]                 setResultUrl(url);
[ 800]                 setStep('done');
[ 801]             } catch (e: any) {
[ 802]                 console.error("Error organizing PDF:", e);
[ 803]                 Alert.alert("Erreur locale", "Échec de l'organisation: " + (e.message || String(e)));
[ 804]                 setStep('organize_editor');
[ 805]             }
[ 806]         }, 300); // Increased timeout to ensure React paints the processing screen
[ 807]     };
[ 808] 
[ 809]     const handleMergeComplete = async (type: 'files' | 'pages', data: any) => {
[ 810]         if (type === 'pages') {
[ 811]             handleOrganizeComplete(data);
[ 812]         } else {
[ 813]             // data is the new filesOrder array
[ 814]             const newSelectedFiles = data.map((item: OrganizeFileItem) => selectedFiles[item.originalIndex]).filter(Boolean);
[ 815]             setSelectedFiles(newSelectedFiles);
[ 816]             
[ 817]             // Fusionner côté client pour éviter les timeouts serveur
[ 818]             setStep('processing');
[ 819]             setFileName('document_fusionne');
[ 820]             
[ 821]             setTimeout(async () => {
[ 822]                 try {
[ 823]                     const newPdfDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
[ 824]                     
[ 825]                     for (const fileItem of data) {
[ 826]                         const sourceFileInfo = organizeFiles.find(f => f.originalIndex === fileItem.originalIndex);
[ 827]                         if (sourceFileInfo) {
[ 828]                             let buffer = sourceFileInfo.buffer;
[ 829]                             if (!buffer) {
[ 830]                                 const originalFile = selectedFiles.find((_, i) => i === fileItem.originalIndex);
[ 831]                                 if (originalFile) {
[ 832]                                     if (Platform.OS === 'web' && originalFile.file) {
[ 833]                                         buffer = await originalFile.file.arrayBuffer();
[ 834]                                     } else {
[ 835]                                         const response = await fetch(originalFile.uri);
[ 836]                                         buffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
[ 837]                                     }
[ 838]                                 }
[ 839]                             }
[ 840]                             if (buffer) {
[ 841]                                 const sourceDoc = await PDFDocument.load(buffer, { ignoreEncryption: true });
[ 842]                                 const copiedPages = await newPdfDoc.copyPages(sourceDoc, sourceDoc.getPageIndices());
[ 843]                                 copiedPages.forEach(page => newPdfDoc.addPage(page));
[ 844]                             }
[ 845]                         }
[ 846]                         
[ 847]                         // Yield main thread to prevent UI freezing on large PDFs
[ 848]                         await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 10));
[ 849]                     }
[ 850]                     
[ 851]                     const finalPdfBytes = await newPdfDoc.save();
[ 852]                     const blob = new Blob([finalPdfBytes], { type: 'application/pdf' });
[ 853]                     const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
[ 854]                     setResultUrl(url);
[ 855]                     setStep('done');
[ 856]                 } catch (e: any) {
[ 857]                     console.error("Error merging files locally:", e);
[ 858]                     setLocalError("Échec de la fusion: " + (e.message || String(e)));
[ 859]                     setStep('merge_editor');
[ 860]                 }
[ 861]             }, 300); // Increased timeout to ensure React paints the processing screen
[ 862]         }
[ 863]     };
[ 864] 
[ 865]     const handleAddFilesToOrganize = async () => {
[ 866]         try {
[ 867]             const res = await DocumentPicker.getDocumentAsync({
[ 868]                 type: 'application/pdf',
[ 869]                 copyToCacheDirectory: false,
[ 870]                 multiple: true
[ 871]             });
[ 872]             if (!res.canceled && res.assets && res.assets.length > 0) {
[ 873]                 setSelectedFiles(prev => [...prev, ...res.assets]);
[ 874]                 await initOrganizeEditor(res.assets, true);
[ 875]             }
[ 876]         } catch (e) {
[ 877]             console.error("Error picking additional files", e);
[ 878]         }
[ 879]     };
[ 880] 
[ 881]     const onDragStart = (e: any, index: number) => {
[ 882]         setDraggedIndex(index);
[ 883]         const dataTransfer = e.dataTransfer || (e.nativeEvent && e.nativeEvent.dataTransfer);
[ 884]         if (dataTransfer) {
[ 885]             dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
[ 886]             dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', index.toString());
[ 887]         }
[ 888]     };
[ 889] 
[ 890]     const onDragOver = (e: any, index: number) => {
[ 891]         e.preventDefault();
[ 892]         const dataTransfer = e.dataTransfer || (e.nativeEvent && e.nativeEvent.dataTransfer);
[ 893]         if (dataTransfer) {
[ 894]             dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
[ 895]         }
[ 896]         if (dragOverIndex !== index) {
[ 897]             setDragOverIndex(index);
[ 898]         }
[ 899]     };
[ 900] 
[ 901]     const onDragLeave = (e: any, index: number) => {
[ 902]         if (dragOverIndex === index) {
[ 903]             setDragOverIndex(null);
[ 904]         }
[ 905]     };
[ 906] 
[ 907]     const onDrop = (e: any, index: number) => {
[ 908]         e.preventDefault();
[ 909]         setDragOverIndex(null);
[ 910]         if (draggedIndex === null || draggedIndex === index) return;
[ 911]         
[ 912]         setSelectedFiles(prev => {
[ 913]             const newFiles = [...prev];
[ 914]             const draggedFile = newFiles[draggedIndex];
[ 915]             const targetFile = newFiles[index];
[ 916]             newFiles[index] = draggedFile;
[ 917]             newFiles[draggedIndex] = targetFile;
[ 918]             return newFiles;
[ 919]         });
[ 920]         setDraggedIndex(null);
[ 921]     };

[ 928]     const handleSplitPDF = async () => {
[ 929]         if (organizeFiles.length === 0) return;
[ 930]         
[ 931]         if (splitTab === 'extract' && extractedPages.length === 0) {
[ 932]             setLocalError("Veuillez sélectionner au moins une page à extraire.");
[ 933]             return;
[ 934]         }
[ 935]         
[ 936]         setIsSplitting(true);
[ 937]         setLocalError(null);
[ 938]         setStep('processing'); // Go to spinner screen
[ 939]         
[ 940]         setTimeout(async () => {
[ 941]             try {
[ 942]                 // Pre-load all source documents to handle multiple files in the visual editor
[ 943]                 const sourceDocs = new Map<number, PDFDocument>();
[ 944]                 for (const fileItem of organizeFiles) {
[ 945]                     if (fileItem.buffer) {
[ 946]                         const doc = await PDFDocument.load(fileItem.buffer.slice(0));
[ 947]                         sourceDocs.set(fileItem.originalIndex, doc);
[ 948]                     }
[ 949]                 }
[ 950]                 
[ 951]                 if (splitTab === 'extract') {
[ 952]                     const newDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
[ 953]                     const pagesToCopy = [...extractedPages].sort((a, b) => a - b).map(idx => organizePages[idx]);
[ 954]                     
[ 955]                     const fileGroups: { fileIndex: number, pageIndices: number[] }[] = [];
[ 956]                     for (const pageItem of pagesToCopy) {
[ 957]                         const lastGroup = fileGroups[fileGroups.length - 1];
[ 958]                         if (lastGroup && lastGroup.fileIndex === pageItem.fileIndex) {
[ 959]                             lastGroup.pageIndices.push(pageItem.pageIndex);
[ 960]                         } else {
[ 961]                             fileGroups.push({ fileIndex: pageItem.fileIndex, pageIndices: [pageItem.pageIndex] });
[ 962]                         }
[ 963]                     }
[ 964]                     
[ 965]                     for (const group of fileGroups) {
[ 966]                         const sourceDoc = sourceDocs.get(group.fileIndex);
[ 967]                         if (sourceDoc) {
[ 968]                             const copiedPages = await newDoc.copyPages(sourceDoc, group.pageIndices);
[ 969]                             for (const copiedPage of copiedPages) {
[ 970]                                 newDoc.addPage(copiedPage);
[ 971]                             }
[ 972]                         }
[ 973]                     }
[ 974]                     
[ 975]                     const pdfBytes = await newDoc.save();
[ 976]                     
[ 977]                     if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 978]                         const blob = new Blob([pdfBytes], { type: 'application/pdf' });
[ 979]                         setResultUrl(URL.createObjectURL(blob));
[ 980]                     }
[ 981]                     setFileName('document_extrait');
[ 982]                     
[ 983]                 } else {
[ 984]                     const zip = new JSZip();
[ 985]                     let docIndex = 1;
[ 986]                     const generatedFiles: {name: string, url: string}[] = [];
[ 987] 
[ 988]                     const totalPages = organizePages.length;
[ 989]                     const chunks: OrganizePageItem[][] = [];
[ 990]                     let currentChunk: OrganizePageItem[] = [];
[ 991]                     
[ 992]                     for (let i = 0; i < totalPages; i++) {
[ 993]                         currentChunk.push(organizePages[i]);
[ 994]                         if (splitPoints.includes(i) || i === totalPages - 1) {
[ 995]                             chunks.push(currentChunk);
[ 996]                             currentChunk = [];
[ 997]                         }
[ 998]                     }
[ 999]                     
[1000]                     for (const chunk of chunks) {
[1001]                         const currentDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
[1002]                         
[1003]                         const fileGroups: { fileIndex: number, pageIndices: number[] }[] = [];
[1004]                         for (const pageItem of chunk) {
[1005]                             const lastGroup = fileGroups[fileGroups.length - 1];
[1006]                             if (lastGroup && lastGroup.fileIndex === pageItem.fileIndex) {
[1007]                                 lastGroup.pageIndices.push(pageItem.pageIndex);
[1008]                             } else {
[1009]                                 fileGroups.push({ fileIndex: pageItem.fileIndex, pageIndices: [pageItem.pageIndex] });
[1010]                             }
[1011]                         }
[1012]                         
[1013]                         for (const group of fileGroups) {
[1014]                             const sourceDoc = sourceDocs.get(group.fileIndex);
[1015]                             if (sourceDoc) {
[1016]                                 const copiedPages = await currentDoc.copyPages(sourceDoc, group.pageIndices);
[1017]                                 for (const copiedPage of copiedPages) {
[1018]                                     currentDoc.addPage(copiedPage);
[1019]                                 }
[1020]                             }
[1021]                         }
[1022] 
[1023]                         const pdfBytes = await currentDoc.save();
[1024]                         const partFileName = `document_partie_${docIndex}.pdf`;
[1025]                         zip.file(partFileName, pdfBytes);
[1026]                         
[1027]                         if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[1028]                             const blob = new Blob([pdfBytes], { type: 'application/pdf' });
[1029]                             generatedFiles.push({
[1030]                                 name: partFileName,
[1031]                                 url: URL.createObjectURL(blob)
[1032]                             });
[1033]                         }
[1034]                         
[1035]                         docIndex++;
[1036]                         await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 10)); // Yield thread between chunks
[1037]                     }
[1038] 
[1039]                     const zipContent = await zip.generateAsync({ type: Platform.OS === 'web' ? 'blob' : 'base64' });
[1040]                     
[1041]                     if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[1042]                         setResultFiles(generatedFiles);
[1043]                         const url = URL.createObjectURL(zipContent as Blob);
[1044]                         setResultUrl(url);
[1045]                     } else {
[1046]                         const fileUri = FileSystem.cacheDirectory + 'documents_divises.zip';
[1047]                         await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(fileUri, zipContent as string, { encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64 });
[1048]                         setResultUrl(fileUri);
[1049]                     }
[1050]                     setFileName('documents_divises.zip');
[1051]                 }
[1052]                 
[1053]                 setStep('done');
[1054]             } catch (e: any) {
[1055]                 console.error("Split error:", e);
[1056]                 setLocalError("Erreur lors du traitement: " + (e.message || String(e)));
[1057]                 setIsSplitting(false);
[1058]                 setStep('split_editor');
[1059]             } finally {
[1060]                 setIsSplitting(false);
[1061]             }
[1062]         }, 100);
[1063]     };
[1064]

[1155]     const processFiles = async (passwordOverride?: string, filesOverride?: any[], compressionLevelOverride?: CompressionLevel) => {
[1156]         const targetFiles = filesOverride || selectedFiles;
[1157]         if (targetFiles.length === 0) return;
[1158]         
[1159]         setStep('processing');
[1160]         
[1161]         let baseName = targetFiles[0].name.split('.').slice(0, -1).join('.');
[1162]         if (targetFiles.length > 1 && selectedService.id === 'merge-pdf') {
[1163]             baseName = 'document_fusionne';
[1164]         } else {
[1165]             baseName += '_converti';
[1166]         }
[1167]         setFileName(baseName);
[1168] 
[1169]         try {
[1170]             const formData = new FormData();
[1171]             
[1172]             if (selectedService.id === 'compress-pdf') {
[1173]                 setProcessingTime(0);
[1174]                 const timerId = setInterval(() => {
[1175]                     setProcessingTime(prev => prev + 1);
[1176]                 }, 1000);
[1177]                 (window as any)._processingTimer = timerId;
[1178]             }
[1179] 
[1180]             for (let i = 0; i < targetFiles.length; i++) {
[1181]                 const file = targetFiles[i];
[1182]                 let blob;
[1183]                 if (Platform.OS === 'web' && file.file) {
[1184]                     blob = file.file;
[1185]                 } else {
[1186]                     const response_file = await fetch(file.uri);
[1187]                     blob = await response_file.blob();
[1188]                 }
[1189]                 
[1190]                 const fieldName = selectedService.multiple ? 'files' : 'file';
[1191]                 formData.append(fieldName, blob, file.name);
[1192]                 
[1193]                 if (!selectedService.multiple) break; 
[1194]             }
[1195] 
[1196]             if (selectedService.id === 'protect-pdf') {
[1197]                 formData.append('password', passwordOverride || pdfPassword || 'faas2024');
[1198]             }
[1199]             if (selectedService.id === 'compress-pdf') {
[1200]                 formData.append('compressionLevel', compressionLevelOverride || compressionLevel);
[1201]             }
[1202]             if (selectedService.id === 'number-pdf') {
[1203]                 formData.append('position', numberingConfig.position);
[1204]                 formData.append('format', numberingConfig.format);
[1205]             }
[1206]             if (selectedService.id === 'ocr-pdf') {
[1207]                 formData.append('lang', ocrLang);
[1208]             }
[1209]             if (selectedService.id === 'pdf-to-image' || selectedService.id === 'image-to-pdf') {
[1210]                 formData.append('quality', conversionQuality);
[1211]             }
[1212] 
[1213]             const controller = new AbortController();
[1214]             // Augmentation du timeout à 5 minutes pour laisser les gros fichiers se traiter
[1215]             const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 300000); 
[1216] 
[1217]             const response = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}${selectedService.endpoint}`, {
[1218]                 method: 'POST',
[1219]                 body: formData,
[1220]                 signal: controller.signal
[1221]             });
[1222] 
[1223]             clearTimeout(timeoutId);
[1224] 
[1225]             if (!response.ok) {
[1226]                 if (response.status === 404) {
[1227]                     throw new Error('NotImplemented');
[1228]                 }
[1229]                 const errorText = await response.text();
[1230]                 throw new Error(errorText || 'Erreur serveur');
[1231]             }
[1232] 
[1233]             let resultBlob;
[1234]             
[1235]             if (selectedService.id === 'compress-pdf') {
[1236]                 const data = await response.json();
[1237]                 if (!data.jobId) throw new Error('Erreur serveur (pas de jobId)');
[1238]                 
[1239]                 const jobId = data.jobId;
[1240]                 
[1241]                 // Boucle de polling (ticket)
[1242]                 let pollingSeconds = 0;
[1243]                 while (true) {
[1244]                     await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 3000));
[1245]                     pollingSeconds += 3;
[1246]                     
[1247]                     // Abort after 30 seconds as requested by the user
[1248]                     if (pollingSeconds >= 30) {
[1249]                         throw new Error("Délai dépassé (30s) : Fichier trop lourd pour notre serveur gratuit. Veuillez utiliser un fichier de moins de 10 Mo.");
[1250]                     }
[1251] 
[1252]                     const statusRes = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/convert/status/${jobId}`);
[1253]                     if (!statusRes.ok) throw new Error('Erreur serveur');
[1254]                     
[1255]                     const statusData = await statusRes.json();
[1256]                     
[1257]                     if (statusData.status === 'done') {
[1258]                         const blobRes = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/convert/download/${jobId}`);
[1259]                         if (!blobRes.ok) throw new Error('Erreur serveur au téléchargement');
[1260]                         resultBlob = await blobRes.blob();
[1261]                         break;
[1262]                     } else if (statusData.status === 'error') {
[1263]                         throw new Error(statusData.error || 'La compression a échoué.');
[1264]                     }
[1265]                     // Si status === 'processing', on attend le prochain tour de boucle
[1266]                 }
[1267]             } else {
[1268]                 // Fonctionnement normal synchrone pour les autres outils
[1269]                 resultBlob = await response.blob();
[1270]             }
[1271] 
[1272]             const url = URL.createObjectURL(resultBlob);
[1273]             setResultUrl(url);
[1274]             setStep('done');
[1275]             if ((window as any)._processingTimer) {
[1276]                 clearInterval((window as any)._processingTimer);
[1277]             }
[1278] 
[1279]         } catch (error: any) {
[1280]             if ((window as any)._processingTimer) {
[1281]                 clearInterval((window as any)._processingTimer);
[1282]             }
[1283]             console.error('CONVERT_ERROR:', error);
[1284]             let errorMsg = error.message && error.message !== 'Erreur serveur' && error.message !== 'Failed to fetch' ? error.message : 'Le traitement a échoué. Vérifiez vos fichiers et réessayez.';
[1285]             if (error.name === 'AbortError' || (error.message && error.message.includes('aborted'))) {
[1286]                 errorMsg = 'Le serveur (hébergement gratuit) met trop de temps à répondre pour ce fichier lourd. Le délai a expiré.';
[1287]             } else if (error.message === 'NotImplemented') {
[1288]                 errorMsg = 'Cette fonctionnalité est en cours de développement et sera disponible prochainement !';
[1289]             }
[1290] 
[1291]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[1292]                 window.alert(errorMsg);
[1293]             } else {
[1294]                 Alert.alert(t('common.error'), errorMsg);
[1295]             }
[1296]             setStep('staging');
[1297]         }
[1298]     };
[1299]

[   2]  * app/history.tsx
[   3]  *
[   4]  * Écran historique des fichiers envoyés et reçus.
[   5]  */
[   6] 
[   7] import { useFocusEffect } from 'expo-router';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   8] import {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   9]     View,
[  10]     Text,
[  11]     Pressable,
[  12]     StyleSheet,
[  13]     ScrollView,
[  14]     ActivityIndicator,
[  15] } from 'react-native';
[  16] import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  17] import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  18] import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  19] import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  20] import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  21] import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  22] import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  23] import { Session } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  24] import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[  25] 
[  26] const STORAGE_KEY = 'faas_history';
[  27] const RECEIVED_STORAGE_KEY = 'faas_received_history';
[  28] 
[  29] type Transfer = {
[  30]     id: string;
[  31]     fileName: string;
[  32]     downloadUrl: string;
[  33]     sentAt: string;
[  34]     status: 'pending' | 'downloaded' | 'expired';
[  35] };
[  36] 
[  37] export default function HistoryScreen() {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Déclaration du composant UI principal de ce fichier.
[  38]     const router = useRouter();
[  39]     const { colors } = useTheme();
[  40]     const { t } = useTranslation();
[  41]     const styles = getStyles(colors);
[  42] 
[  43]     const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'sent' | 'received'>('sent');
[  44]     const [sentFiles, setSentFiles] = useState<Transfer[]>([]);
[  45]     const [receivedFiles, setReceivedFiles] = useState<Transfer[]>([]);
[  46]     const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
[  47]     const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
[  48] 
[  49]     useFocusEffect(
[  50]         useCallback(() => {
[  51]             supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session } }) => {
[  52]                 setSession(session);
[  53]                 if (session) {
[  54]                     loadHistory();
[  55]                 } else {
[  56]                     setLoading(false);
[  57]                 }
[  58]             });
[  59]         }, [])
[  60]     );
[  61] 
[  62]     const loadHistory = async () => {
[  63]         try {
[  64]             const [sentData, receivedData] = await Promise.all([
[  65]                 AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY),
[  66]                 AsyncStorage.getItem(RECEIVED_STORAGE_KEY)
[  67]             ]);
[  68]             
[  69]             if (sentData) {
[  70]                 setSentFiles(JSON.parse(sentData));
[  71]             }
[  72]             if (receivedData) {
[  73]                 setReceivedFiles(JSON.parse(receivedData));
[  74]             }
[  75]         } catch (error) {
[  76]             console.log('Erreur chargement historique:', error);
[  77]         } finally {
[  78]             setLoading(false);
[  79]         }
[  80]     };
[  81] 
[  82]     const deleteTransfer = async (id: string, type: 'sent' | 'received' = 'sent') => {
[  83]         try {
[  84]             if (type === 'sent') {
[  85]                 const updated = sentFiles.filter(f => f.id !== id);
[  86]                 setSentFiles(updated);
[  87]                 await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
[  88]             } else {
[  89]                 const updated = receivedFiles.filter(f => f.id !== id);
[  90]                 setReceivedFiles(updated);
[  91]                 await AsyncStorage.setItem(RECEIVED_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
[  92]             }
[  93]         } catch (error) {
[  94]             console.log('Erreur suppression:', error);
[  95]         }
[  96]     };
[  97] 
[  98]     const getStatusStyle = (status: Transfer['status']) => {
[  99]         switch (status) {
[ 100]             case 'downloaded':
[ 101]                 return { color: colors.success, icon: 'checkmark-circle-outline' };
[ 102]             case 'expired':
[ 103]                 return { color: colors.danger, icon: 'time-outline' };
[ 104]             default:
[ 105]                 return { color: colors.primary, icon: 'hourglass-outline' };
[ 106]         }
[ 107]     };
[ 108] 
[ 109]     const getStatusLabel = (status: Transfer['status']) => {
[ 110]         switch (status) {
[ 111]             case 'downloaded': return t('history.status_downloaded');
[ 112]             case 'expired': return t('history.status_expired');
[ 113]             default: return t('history.status_pending');
[ 114]         }
[ 115]     };
[ 116] 
[ 117]     return (
[ 118]         <SafeAreaView style={styles.container}>
[ 119]             <View style={styles.backgroundGlow} pointerEvents="none" />
[ 120] 
[ 121]             <View style={styles.contentWrapper}>
[ 122]                 <Pressable 
[ 123]                     style={({ pressed, hovered }: any) => [
[ 124]                         styles.backButton,
[ 125]                         (pressed || hovered) && styles.backButtonHovered
[ 126]                     ]}
[ 127]                     onPress={() => router.push('/')}>
[ 128]                     <Ionicons name="arrow-back-outline" size={18} color={colors.textMuted} />
[ 129]                     <Text style={styles.backButtonText}>{t('common.back')}</Text>
[ 130]                 </Pressable>
[ 131] 
[ 132]                 <View style={styles.headerContainer}>
[ 133]                     <Text style={styles.title}>{t('history.title')}</Text>
[ 134]                     <Text style={styles.subtitle}>{t('history.subtitle')}</Text>
[ 135]                 </View>
[ 136] 
[ 137]                 <View style={styles.tabs}>
[ 138]                     <Pressable
[ 139]                         style={[styles.tab, activeTab === 'sent' && styles.tabActive]}
[ 140]                         onPress={() => setActiveTab('sent')}>
[ 141]                         <Text style={[styles.tabText, activeTab === 'sent' && styles.tabTextActive]}>
[ 142]                             {t('history.sent')}
[ 143]                         </Text>
[ 144]                     </Pressable>
[ 145]                     <Pressable
[ 146]                         style={[styles.tab, activeTab === 'received' && styles.tabActive]}
[ 147]                         onPress={() => setActiveTab('received')}>
[ 148]                         <Text style={[styles.tabText, activeTab === 'received' && styles.tabTextActive]}>
[ 149]                             {t('history.received')}
[ 150]                         </Text>
[ 151]                     </Pressable>
[ 152]                 </View>
[ 153] 
[ 154]                 {!loading && !session ? (
[ 155]                     <View style={styles.premiumGate}>
[ 156]                         <View style={styles.premiumIconContainer}>
[ 157]                             <Ionicons name="lock-closed-outline" size={48} color={colors.primary} />
[ 158]                         </View>
[ 159]                         <Text style={styles.premiumTitle}>{t('history.premium_title')}</Text>
[ 160]                         <Text style={styles.premiumDesc}>
[ 161]                             {t('history.premium_desc')}
[ 162]                         </Text>
[ 163]                         <Pressable 
[ 164]                             style={styles.premiumButton} 
[ 165]                             onPress={() => router.push('/auth')}>
[ 166]                             <Text style={styles.premiumButtonText}>{t('auth.signup')}</Text>
[ 167]                         </Pressable>
[ 168]                     </View>
[ 169]                 ) : loading ? (
[ 170]                     <View style={styles.emptyContainer}>
[ 171]                         <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
[ 172]                     </View>
[ 173]                 ) : activeTab === 'sent' ? (
[ 174]                     <View style={styles.listContainer}>
[ 175]                         <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
[ 176]                             {sentFiles.length === 0 ? (
[ 177]                                 <View style={styles.emptyContainer}>
[ 178]                                     <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={64} color={colors.border} />
[ 179]                                     <Text style={styles.emptyText}>{t('history.no_sent')}</Text>
[ 180]                                 </View>
[ 181]                             ) : (
[ 182]                                 sentFiles.map((transfer) => {
[ 183]                                     const statusStyle = getStatusStyle(transfer.status);
[ 184]                                     return (
[ 185]                                         <View key={transfer.id} style={styles.transferCard}>
[ 186]                                             <View style={styles.fileIcon}>
[ 187]                                                 <Ionicons name="document-outline" size={24} color={colors.primary} />
[ 188]                                             </View>
[ 189]                                             <View style={styles.fileInfo}>
[ 190]                                                 <Text style={styles.fileName} numberOfLines={1}>
[ 191]                                                     {transfer.fileName}
[ 192]                                                 </Text>
[ 193]                                                 <Text style={styles.fileDate}>{transfer.sentAt}</Text>
[ 194]                                                 <View style={styles.statusRow}>
[ 195]                                                     <Ionicons
[ 196]                                                         name={statusStyle.icon as any}
[ 197]                                                         size={14}
[ 198]                                                         color={statusStyle.color}
[ 199]                                                     />
[ 200]                                                     <Text style={[styles.statusText, { color: statusStyle.color }]}>
[ 201]                                                         {getStatusLabel(transfer.status)}
[ 202]                                                     </Text>
[ 203]                                                 </View>
[ 204]                                             </View>
[ 205]                                             <Pressable
[ 206]                                                 onPress={() => deleteTransfer(transfer.id, 'sent')}
[ 207]                                                 style={styles.deleteButton}>
[ 208]                                                 <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={20} color={colors.textMuted} />
[ 209]                                             </Pressable>
[ 210]                                         </View>
[ 211]                                     );
[ 212]                                 })
[ 213]                             )}
[ 214]                         </ScrollView>
[ 215]                     </View>
[ 216]                 ) : (
[ 217]                     <View style={styles.listContainer}>
[ 218]                         <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
[ 219]                             {receivedFiles.length === 0 ? (
[ 220]                                 <View style={styles.emptyContainer}>
[ 221]                                     <Ionicons name="download-outline" size={64} color={colors.border} />
[ 222]                                     <Text style={styles.emptyText}>{t('history.no_received')}</Text>
[ 223]                                 </View>
[ 224]                             ) : (
[ 225]                                 receivedFiles.map((transfer) => {
[ 226]                                     const statusStyle = getStatusStyle(transfer.status);
[ 227]                                     return (
[ 228]                                         <View key={transfer.id} style={styles.transferCard}>
[ 229]                                             <View style={styles.fileIcon}>
[ 230]                                                 <Ionicons name="document-outline" size={24} color={colors.success} />
[ 231]                                             </View>
[ 232]                                             <View style={styles.fileInfo}>
[ 233]                                                 <Text style={styles.fileName} numberOfLines={1}>
[ 234]                                                     {transfer.fileName}
[ 235]                                                 </Text>
[ 236]                                                 <Text style={styles.fileDate}>{transfer.sentAt}</Text>
[ 237]                                                 <View style={styles.statusRow}>
[ 238]                                                     <Ionicons
[ 239]                                                         name={statusStyle.icon as any}
[ 240]                                                         size={14}
[ 241]                                                         color={statusStyle.color}
[ 242]                                                     />
[ 243]                                                     <Text style={[styles.statusText, { color: statusStyle.color }]}>
[ 244]                                                         {getStatusLabel(transfer.status)}
[ 245]                                                     </Text>
[ 246]                                                 </View>
[ 247]                                             </View>
[ 248]                                             <Pressable
[ 249]                                                 onPress={() => deleteTransfer(transfer.id, 'received')}
[ 250]                                                 style={styles.deleteButton}>
[ 251]                                                 <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={20} color={colors.textMuted} />

[ 260]             </View>

        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.

        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   3] import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   4] import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   5] import PdfEditor from '../../components/PdfEditor';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   6] import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
