[   9] const SERVER_URL = __DEV__ ? 'http://localhost:3000' : 'https://faas-transfer.onrender.com';
[  10] 
[  11] export default function SignTokenScreen() {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Déclaration du composant UI principal de ce fichier.
[  12]     const { token } = useLocalSearchParams();
[  13]     const router = useRouter();
[  14]     const { colors } = useTheme();
[  15]     
[  16]     const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
[  17]     const [requestData, setRequestData] = useState<any>(null);
[  18]     const [pdfPages, setPdfPages] = useState<string[]>([]);
[  19]     const [error, setError] = useState('');
[  20]     const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
[  21]         const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
[  22]     const [finalFileUrl, setFinalFileUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
[  23] 
[  24]     useEffect(() => {
[  25]         if (!token) return;
[  26]         fetchRequestData();
[  27]     }, [token]);
[  28] 
[  29]     const fetchRequestData = async () => {
[  30]         try {
[  31]             setLoading(true);
[  32]             const res = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/signature/request/${token}`);
[  33]             if (!res.ok) {
[  34]                 const errData = await res.json();
[  35]                 throw new Error(errData.error || "Demande introuvable");
[  36]             }
[  37]             const { request } = await res.json();
[  38]             
[  39]             if (request.status === 'signed') {
[  40]                 if (request.signature_documents && request.signature_documents.final_file_url) {
[  41]                     setFinalFileUrl(request.signature_documents.final_file_url);
[  42]                 }
[  43]                 setDone(true);
[  44]                 setLoading(false);
[  45]                 return;
[  46]             }
[  47] 
[  48]             setRequestData(request);
[  49]             
[  50]             // Generate PDF images for PdfEditor
[  51]             await loadPdfImages(request.signature_documents.original_file_url);
[  52]             
[  53]         } catch (err: any) {
[  54]             console.error(err);
[  55]             setError(err.message || "Une erreur est survenue");
[  56]         } finally {
[  57]             setLoading(false);
[  58]         }
[  59]     };
[  60] 
[  61]     const loadPdfImages = async (pdfUrl: string) => {
[  62]         try {
[  63]             // 1. Download PDF
[  64]             const pdfRes = await fetch(pdfUrl);
[  65]             if (!pdfRes.ok) throw new Error("Impossible de télécharger le PDF");
[  66]             const pdfBlob = await pdfRes.blob();
[  67]             
[  68]             // 2. Convert to images via backend
[  69]             const formData = new FormData();
[  70]             formData.append('file', pdfBlob, 'document.pdf');
[  71]             
[  72]             const convRes = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/convert/pdf-to-image`, {
[  73]                 method: 'POST',
[  74]                 body: formData
[  75]             });
[  76]             
[  77]             if (!convRes.ok) throw new Error("Erreur de conversion");
[  78]             
[  79]             const contentType = convRes.headers.get('content-type');
[  80]             const blob = await convRes.blob();
[  81]             const pages: string[] = [];
[  82]             
[  83]             if (contentType?.includes('zip')) {
[  84]                 const zip = new JSZip();
[  85]                 const unzipped = await zip.loadAsync(blob);
[  86]                 const fileNames = Object.keys(unzipped.files).sort();
[  87]                 for (const filename of fileNames) {
[  88]                     const f = unzipped.files[filename];
[  89]                     if (!f.dir) {
[  90]                         const imgBlob = await f.async('blob');
[  91]                         pages.push(URL.createObjectURL(imgBlob));
[  92]                     }
[  93]                 }
[  94]             } else {
[  95]                 pages.push(URL.createObjectURL(blob));
[  96]             }
[  97]             
[  98]             setPdfPages(pages);
[  99]         } catch (err: any) {
[ 100]             console.error(err);
[ 101]             throw new Error("Erreur lors de l'initialisation du document: " + err.message);
[ 102]         }
[ 103]     };
[ 104] 
[ 105]     const handleComplete = async (edits: any[]) => {
[ 106]         // We only care about signatures
[ 107]         const signatures = edits.filter(e => e.type === 'signature' && e.signatureData);
[ 108]         if (signatures.length === 0) {
[ 109]             const msg = "Veuillez ajouter votre signature avant de valider.";
[ 110]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') window.alert(msg);
[ 111]             else Alert.alert("Erreur", msg);
[ 112]             return;
[ 113]         }
[ 114] 
[ 115]         setSubmitting(true);
[ 116]         try {
[ 117]             // Take the first signature (or send all if needed, but let's assume 1)
[ 118]             const sig = signatures[0];
[ 119]             
[ 120]             // Inject box width and height into signatureData
[ 121]             const signaturePayload = {
[ 122]                 ...sig.signatureData,
[ 123]                 boxWidth: sig.width || 20,
[ 124]                 boxHeight: sig.height || 10
[ 125]             };
[ 126]             const signatureStr = JSON.stringify(signaturePayload);
[ 127] 
[ 128]             const res = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/signature/complete-request`, {
[ 129]                 method: 'POST',
[ 130]                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
[ 131]                 body: JSON.stringify({
[ 132]                     token,
[ 133]                     signatureBase64: signatureStr,
[ 134]                     x: sig.x || 0,
[ 135]                     y: sig.y || 0,
[ 136]                     pageIndex: sig.pageIndex || 0
[ 137]                 })
[ 138]             });
[ 139] 
[ 140]             if (!res.ok) {
[ 141]                 const errData = await res.json();
[ 142]                 throw new Error(errData.error || "Erreur de validation");
[ 143]             }
[ 144]             
[ 145]             const resData = await res.json();
[ 146]             if (resData.finalFileUrl) {
[ 147]                 setFinalFileUrl(resData.finalFileUrl);
[ 148]                 // Open automatically
[ 149]                 if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 150]                     window.open(resData.finalFileUrl, '_blank');
[ 151]                 }
[ 152]             }
[ 153]             setDone(true);
[ 154]         } catch (err: any) {
[ 155]             console.error(err);
[ 156]             if (Platform.OS === 'web') window.alert(err.message);
[ 157]             else Alert.alert("Erreur", err.message);
[ 158]         } finally {
[ 159]             setSubmitting(false);
[ 160]         }
[ 161]     };
[ 162] 
[ 163]     if (loading || submitting) {
[ 164]         return (
[ 165]             <SafeAreaView style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center' }]}>
[ 166]                 <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
[ 167]                 <Text style={{ color: colors.text, marginTop: 16 }}>
[ 168]                     {submitting ? "Enregistrement de la signature..." : "Chargement du document..."}
[ 169]                 </Text>
[ 170]             </SafeAreaView>
[ 171]         );
[ 172]     }
[ 173] 
[ 174]     if (error) {
[ 175]         return (
[ 176]             <SafeAreaView style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', padding: 20 }]}>
[ 177]                 <Ionicons name="alert-circle-outline" size={64} color={colors.danger} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }} />
[ 178]                 <Text style={{ color: colors.text, fontSize: 18, textAlign: 'center' }}>{error}</Text>
[ 179]             </SafeAreaView>
[ 180]         );
[ 181]     }
[ 182] 
[ 183]     if (done) {
[ 184]         return (
[ 185]             <SafeAreaView style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', padding: 20 }]}>
[ 186]                 <View style={{ width: 80, height: 80, borderRadius: 40, backgroundColor: colors.success + '20', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', marginBottom: 24 }}>
[ 187]                     <Ionicons name="checkmark-circle" size={48} color={colors.success} />
[ 188]                 </View>
[ 189]                 <Text style={{ color: colors.text, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: 8 }}>C'est tout bon !</Text>
[ 190]                 <Text style={{ color: colors.textMuted, fontSize: 16, textAlign: 'center', maxWidth: 400, marginBottom: 24 }}>
[ 191]                     Votre signature a été enregistrée avec succès.
[ 192]                 </Text>
[ 193]                 {finalFileUrl && (
[ 194]                     <a 
[ 195]                         href={finalFileUrl} 
[ 196]                         target="_blank" 
[ 197]                         rel="noopener noreferrer"
[ 198]                         style={{
[ 199]                             backgroundColor: colors.primary,
[ 200]                             padding: '12px 24px',
[ 201]                             borderRadius: '12px',
[ 202]                             color: 'white',
[ 203]                             textDecoration: 'none',
[ 204]                             fontWeight: 'bold',
[ 205]                             display: 'flex',
[ 206]                             alignItems: 'center',
[ 207]                             gap: '8px'
[ 208]                         }}
[ 209]                     >
[ 210]                         <Ionicons name="download-outline" size={20} color="white" />
[ 211]                         Télécharger le document final
[ 212]                     </a>
[ 213]                 )}
[ 214]             </SafeAreaView>
[ 215]         );
[ 216]     }
[ 217] 
[ 218]     if (pdfPages.length > 0) {
[ 219]         return (
[ 220]             <PdfEditor
[ 221]                 pages={pdfPages}
[ 222]                 onComplete={handleComplete}
[ 223]                 onCancel={() => {
[ 224]                     const msg = "Êtes-vous sûr de vouloir annuler ?";
[ 225]                     if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 226]                         if (window.confirm(msg)) window.close();
[ 227]                     } else {
[ 228]                         Alert.alert("Annuler", msg, [
[ 229]                             { text: "Non" },
[ 230]                             { text: "Oui", style: "destructive", onPress: () => router.back() }
[ 231]                         ]);
[ 232]                     }
[ 233]                 }}
[ 234]                 colors={colors}
[ 235]                 autoOpenSignTool={true}
[ 236]                 restrictedMode={true}
[ 237]             />
[ 238]         );
[ 239]     }
[ 240] 
[ 241]     return null;
[ 242] }
[ 243]

[ 245]     container: {
[ 246]         flex: 1,
[ 247]     }

[  31] const SERVER_URL = __DEV__ ? 'http://localhost:3000' : 'https://faas-transfer.onrender.com';
[  32] 
[  33] export default function ReceiveScreen() {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Déclaration du composant UI principal de ce fichier.
[  34]     const router = useRouter();
[  35]     const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
[  36]     const { t } = useTranslation();
[  37]     const styles = getStyles(colors, isDark);
[  38] 
[  39]     const [step, setStep] = useState<'choice' | 'scanning' | 'manual' | 'downloading' | 'preview' | 'done'>('choice');
[  40]     const [previewData, setPreviewData] = useState<any>(null);
[  41]     const [link, setLink] = useState('');
[  42]     const [fileName, setFileName] = useState('');
[  43]     const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();
[  44]     const fadeAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
[  45] 
[  46]     useEffect(() => {
[  47]         if (step === 'preview') {
[  48]             Animated.timing(fadeAnim, {
[  49]                 toValue: 1,
[  50]                 duration: 400,
[  51]                 useNativeDriver: true,
[  52]             }).start();
[  53]         } else {
[  54]             fadeAnim.setValue(0);
[  55]         }
[  56]     }, [step, fadeAnim]);
[  57] 
[  58]     const extractId = (url: string) => {
[  59]         const parts = url.split('/download/');
[  60]         return parts.length > 1 ? parts[1].trim() : null;
[  61]     };
[  62] 
[  63]     const downloadFile = async (url: string) => {
[  64]         setStep('downloading');
[  65]         const id = extractId(url);
[  66] 
[  67]         if (!id) {
[  68]             Alert.alert(t('common.error'), 'Le lien est invalide. Vérifiez et réessayez.');
[  69]             setStep('choice');
[  70]             return;
[  71]         }
[  72] 
[  73]         try {
[  74]             const res = await fetch(`${SERVER_URL}/download/${id}/details`);
[  75]             if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Introuvable');
[  76]             const data = await res.json();
[  77]             setPreviewData({ ...data, id });
[  78]             setStep('preview');
[  79]         } catch (error) {
[  80]             Alert.alert(
[  81]                 t('common.error'),
[  82]                 'Le téléchargement a échoué. Le lien est peut-être expiré.'
[  83]             );
[  84]             setStep('choice');
[  85]         }
[  86]     };
[  87] 
[  88]     const performRealDownload = async () => {
[  89]         if (!previewData?.id) return;
[  90]         const id = previewData.id;
[  91]         try {
[  92]             const downloadUrl = `${SERVER_URL}/download/${id}?zip=true`;
[  93]             const supported = await Linking.canOpenURL(downloadUrl);
[  94]             if (!supported) throw new Error('URL non supportée');
[  95] 
[  96]             await Linking.openURL(downloadUrl);
[  97] 
[  98]             setFileName(id);
[  99]             setStep('done');
[ 100] 
[ 101]             const receivedTransfer = {
[ 102]                 id: id,
[ 103]                 fileName: previewData.fileName || `Fichier reçu (${id})`,
[ 104]                 downloadUrl: downloadUrl,
[ 105]                 sentAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', {
[ 106]                     day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
[ 107]                     hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'
[ 108]                 }),
[ 109]                 status: 'downloaded',
[ 110]             };
[ 111] 
[ 112]             const existing = await AsyncStorage.getItem('faas_received_history');
[ 113]             const history = existing ? JSON.parse(existing) : [];
[ 114]             if (!history.find((t: any) => t.id === id)) {

[  16] interface MergeEditorProps {
[  17]     pages: OrganizePageItem[];
[  18]     files: OrganizeFileItem[];
[  19]     onComplete: (type: 'files' | 'pages', data: any) => void;
[  20]     onCancel: () => void;
[  21]     onAddFiles: () => void;
[  22]     colors: any;
[  23] }
[  24] 
[  25] export default function MergeEditor({ 
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Déclaration du composant UI principal de ce fichier.
[  26]     pages: initialPages, 
[  27]     files: initialFiles, 
[  28]     onComplete, 
[  29]     onCancel, 
[  30]     onAddFiles,
[  31]     colors 
[  32] }: MergeEditorProps) {
[  33]     const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'fichiers' | 'pages'>('fichiers');
[  34]     
[  35]     const [pages, setPages] = useState<OrganizePageItem[]>(initialPages);
[  36]     const [filesOrder, setFilesOrder] = useState<OrganizeFileItem[]>(initialFiles);
[  37] 
[  38]     useEffect(() => {
[  39]         setPages(prev => {
[  40]             const newPages = [...prev];
[  41]             let changed = false;
[  42]             initialPages.forEach(p => {
[  43]                 if (!newPages.find(existing => existing.id === p.id)) {
[  44]                     newPages.push(p);
[  45]                     changed = true;
[  46]                 }
[  47]             });
[  48]             if (changed) {
[  49]                 newPages.sort((a, b) => {
[  50]                     if (a.fileIndex !== b.fileIndex) return a.fileIndex - b.fileIndex;
[  51]                     return a.pageIndex - b.pageIndex;
[  52]                 });
[  53]             }
[  54]             return changed ? newPages : prev;
[  55]         });
[  56]     }, [initialPages]);
[  57] 
[  58]     useEffect(() => {
[  59]         setFilesOrder(prev => {
[  60]             const newFiles = [...prev];
[  61]             let changed = false;
[  62]             initialFiles.forEach(f => {
[  63]                 const existing = newFiles.find(existing => existing.originalIndex === f.originalIndex);
[  64]                 if (!existing) {
[  65]                     newFiles.push(f);
[  66]                     changed = true;
[  67]                 } else if (existing.pageCount !== f.pageCount) {
[  68]                     existing.pageCount = f.pageCount;
[  69]                     changed = true;
[  70]                 }
[  71]             });
[  72]             return changed ? newFiles : prev;
[  73]         });
[  74]     }, [initialFiles]);
[  75]     
[  76]     const [draggedPage, setDraggedPage] = useState<number | null>(null);
[  77]     const [dragOverPage, setDragOverPage] = useState<number | null>(null);
[  78] 
[  79]     const [draggedFile, setDraggedFile] = useState<number | null>(null);
[  80]     const [dragOverFile, setDragOverFile] = useState<number | null>(null);
[  81] 
[  82]     // --- DRAG & DROP FOR PAGES ---
[  83]     const onPageDragStart = (e: any, index: number) => {
[  84]         setDraggedPage(index);
[  85]         const dataTransfer = e.nativeEvent?.dataTransfer || e.dataTransfer;
[  86]         if (dataTransfer) {
[  87]             dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
[  88]             dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', index.toString());
[  89]         }
[  90]     };
[  91] 
[  92]     const onPageDragOver = (e: any, index: number) => {
[  93]         e.preventDefault();
[  94]         setDragOverPage(index);
[  95]     };
[  96] 
[  97]     const onPageDrop = (e: any, targetIndex: number) => {
[  98]         e.preventDefault();
[  99]         setDragOverPage(null);
[ 100]         if (draggedPage === null) return;
[ 101]         if (draggedPage === targetIndex) return;
[ 102] 
[ 103]         setPages(prev => {
[ 104]             const next = [...prev];
[ 105]             const item = next.splice(draggedPage, 1)[0];
[ 106]             next.splice(targetIndex, 0, item);
[ 107]             return next;
[ 108]         });
[ 109]         setDraggedPage(null);
[ 110]     };
[ 111] 
[ 112]     // --- DRAG & DROP FOR FILES ---
[ 113]     const onFileDragStart = (e: any, index: number) => {
[ 114]         setDraggedFile(index);
[ 115]         const dataTransfer = e.nativeEvent?.dataTransfer || e.dataTransfer;
[ 116]         if (dataTransfer) {
[ 117]             dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
[ 118]             dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', index.toString());
[ 119]         }
[ 120]     };
[ 121] 
[ 122]     const onFileDragOver = (e: any, index: number) => {
[ 123]         e.preventDefault();
[ 124]         setDragOverFile(index);
[ 125]     };
[ 126] 
[ 127]     const onFileDrop = (e: any, targetIndex: number) => {
[ 128]         e.preventDefault();
[ 129]         setDragOverFile(null);
[ 130]         if (draggedFile === null) return;
[ 131]         if (draggedFile === targetIndex) return;
[ 132] 
[ 133]         setFilesOrder(prev => {
[ 134]             const next = [...prev];
[ 135]             const item = next.splice(draggedFile, 1)[0];
[ 136]             next.splice(targetIndex, 0, item);
[ 137]             
[ 138]             reorderPagesByFiles(next);
[ 139]             
[ 140]             return next;
[ 141]         });
[ 142]         setDraggedFile(null);
[ 143]     };
[ 144] 
[ 145]     const reorderPagesByFiles = (newFilesOrder: OrganizeFileItem[]) => {
[ 146]         setPages(prev => {
[ 147]             const groupedPages = new Map<number, OrganizePageItem[]>();
[ 148]             prev.forEach(p => {
[ 149]                 if (!groupedPages.has(p.fileIndex)) {
[ 150]                     groupedPages.set(p.fileIndex, []);
[ 151]                 }
[ 152]                 groupedPages.get(p.fileIndex)!.push(p);
[ 153]             });
[ 154] 
[ 155]             const newPages: OrganizePageItem[] = [];
[ 156]             newFilesOrder.forEach(file => {
[ 157]                 const pagesForFile = groupedPages.get(file.originalIndex);
[ 158]                 if (pagesForFile) {
[ 159]                     newPages.push(...pagesForFile);
[ 160]                 }
[ 161]             });
[ 162]             return newPages;
[ 163]         });
[ 164]     };
[ 165] 
[ 166]     const removeFile = (originalIndex: number) => {
[ 167]         setFilesOrder(prev => prev.filter(f => f.originalIndex !== originalIndex));
[ 168]         setPages(prev => prev.filter(p => p.fileIndex !== originalIndex));
[ 169]     };
[ 170] 
[ 171]     const removePage = (id: string) => {
[ 172]         setPages(prev => prev.filter(p => p.id !== id));
[ 173]     };
[ 174] 
[ 175]     const handleDone = () => {
[ 176]         if (activeTab === 'fichiers') {
[ 177]             onComplete('files', filesOrder);
[ 178]         } else {
[ 179]             onComplete('pages', pages);
[ 180]         }
[ 181]     };
[ 182] 
[ 183]     return (
[ 184]         <SafeAreaView style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
[ 185]             {/* TOOLBAR */}

[ 187]                 <View style={styles.toolbarLeft}>

[ 189]                         <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={24} color={colors.text} />
[ 190]                     </Pressable>
[ 191]                     <Text style={[styles.toolbarTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>Fusionner</Text>
[ 192]                     
[ 193]                     <View style={styles.tabContainer}>
[ 194]                         <Pressable 
[ 195]                             style={[styles.tab, activeTab === 'fichiers' && styles.activeTab, activeTab === 'fichiers' && { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}
[ 196]                             onPress={() => setActiveTab('fichiers')}
[ 197]                         >
[ 198]                             <Ionicons name="document-outline" size={16} color={activeTab === 'fichiers' ? '#fff' : colors.text} />
[ 199]                             <Text style={[styles.tabText, { color: activeTab === 'fichiers' ? '#fff' : colors.text }]}>Fichiers</Text>
[ 200]                         </Pressable>
[ 201]                         <Pressable 
[ 202]                             style={[styles.tab, activeTab === 'pages' && styles.activeTab, activeTab === 'pages' && { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}
[ 203]                             onPress={() => setActiveTab('pages')}
[ 204]                         >
[ 205]                             <Ionicons name="documents-outline" size={16} color={activeTab === 'pages' ? '#fff' : colors.text} />

--- FICHIER : components/PdfThumbnail.web.tsx (84 lignes) ---
Ce fichier est un pilier de l'architecture. Voici son contenu intégral avec documentation détaillée :

[   1] import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   2] import { View, ActivityIndicator } from 'react-native';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   3] 
[   4] let pdfjsLib: any = null;
[   5] 
[   6] const initPdfJs = async () => {
[   7]     if (pdfjsLib) return pdfjsLib;
[   8]     return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
[   9]         const script = document.createElement('script');
[  10]         script.src = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/2.16.105/pdf.min.js';
[  11]         script.onload = () => {
[  12]             const pdfjs = (window as any).pdfjsLib;
[  13]             pdfjs.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/2.16.105/pdf.worker.min.js';
[  14]             pdfjsLib = pdfjs;
[  15]             resolve(pdfjs);
[  16]         };
[  17]         script.onerror = reject;
[  18]         document.head.appendChild(script);
[  19]     });
[  20] };
[  21] 
[  22] export default function PdfThumbnail({ fileUri, pageIndex, style }: { fileUri: string, pageIndex: number, style?: any }) {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Déclaration du composant UI principal de ce fichier.
[  23]     const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
[  24]     const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
[  25]     const [error, setError] = useState(false);
[  26] 
[  27]     useEffect(() => {
[  28]         let active = true;
[  29]         
[  30]         const renderPage = async () => {
[  31]             try {
[  32]                 const pdfjs = await initPdfJs();
[  33]                 
[  34]                 const loadingTask = pdfjs.getDocument(fileUri);
[  35]                 const pdf = await loadingTask.promise;
[  36]                 
[  37]                 if (!active) return;
[  38]                 
[  39]                 const page = await pdf.getPage(pageIndex + 1);
[  40]                 if (!active) return;
[  41]                 
[  42]                 const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 0.5 });
[  43]                 
[  44]                 const canvas = canvasRef.current;
[  45]                 if (!canvas) return;
[  46]                 
[  47]                 const context = canvas.getContext('2d');
[  48]                 if (!context) return;
[  49]                 
[  50]                 canvas.height = viewport.height;
[  51]                 canvas.width = viewport.width;
[  52]                 
[  53]                 const renderContext = {
[  54]                     canvasContext: context,
[  55]                     viewport: viewport
[  56]                 };
[  57]                 await page.render(renderContext).promise;
[  58]                 if (active) {
[  59]                     setLoading(false);
[  60]                 }
[  61]             } catch (e) {
[  62]                 console.error("Erreur thumbnail PDF:", e);
[  63]                 if (active) {
[  64]                     setError(true);
[  65]                     setLoading(false);
[  66]                 }
[  67]             }
[  68]         };
[  69]         
[  70]         renderPage();
[  71]         
[  72]         return () => { active = false; };
[  73]     }, [fileUri, pageIndex]);
[  74] 
[  75]     return (
[  76]         <View style={[{ width: '100%', height: '100%', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center' }, style]}>
[  77]             {/* @ts-ignore */}
[  78]             <canvas ref={canvasRef} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'contain', display: loading || error ? 'none' : 'block' }} />
[  79]             {loading && <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#4F46E5" />}
[  80]             {error && <View style={{ width: 24, height: 24, backgroundColor: '#ff4444', borderRadius: 12 }} />}
[  81]         </View>
[  82]     );
[  83] }
[  84]

[  13] interface RotationEditorProps {
[  14]     pages: string[];

[  17]     colors: any;
[  18] }
[  19] 
[  20] export default function RotationEditor({ pages, onComplete, onCancel, colors }: RotationEditorProps) {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Déclaration du composant UI principal de ce fichier.
[  21]     // Array to hold the rotation angle (0, 90, 180, 270) for each page
[  22]     const [rotations, setRotations] = useState<number[]>(pages.map(() => 0));
[  23] 
[  24]     const rotateAll = (direction: 'left' | 'right') => {
[  25]         setRotations(prev => prev.map(angle => {
[  26]             let newAngle = direction === 'right' ? angle + 90 : angle - 90;
[  27]             if (newAngle >= 360) newAngle -= 360;
[  28]             if (newAngle < 0) newAngle += 360;
[  29]             return newAngle;
[  30]         }));
[  31]     };
[  32] 
[  33]     const rotatePage = (index: number) => {
[  34]         setRotations(prev => {
[  35]             const next = [...prev];
[  36]             next[index] = (next[index] + 90) % 360;
[  37]             return next;
[  38]         });
[  39]     };
[  40] 
[  41]     return (
[  42]         <SafeAreaView style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
[  43]             {/* TOOLBAR */}
[  44]             <View style={[styles.toolbar, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
[  45]                 <Pressable onPress={onCancel} style={styles.backButton}>
[  46]                     <Ionicons name="close" size={28} color={colors.text} />
[  47]                 </Pressable>
[  48] 
[  49]                 <View style={styles.toolsContainer}>
[  50]                     <Pressable 
[  51]                         style={({ pressed, hovered }: any) => [
[  52]                             styles.toolButton,
[  53]                             { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)' },
[  54]                             (pressed || hovered) && { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)' }
[  55]                         ]}
[  56]                         onPress={() => rotateAll('left')}
[  57]                     >
[  58]                         <Ionicons name="arrow-undo-outline" size={20} color={colors.text} />
[  59]                         <Text style={[styles.toolButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>Tout à gauche</Text>
[  60]                     </Pressable>
[  61]                     
[  62]                     <Pressable 
[  63]                         style={({ pressed, hovered }: any) => [
[  64]                             styles.toolButton,
[  65]                             { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)' },
[  66]                             (pressed || hovered) && { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)' }
[  67]                         ]}
[  68]                         onPress={() => rotateAll('right')}
[  69]                     >
[  70]                         <Ionicons name="arrow-redo-outline" size={20} color={colors.text} />
[  71]                         <Text style={[styles.toolButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>Tout à droite</Text>
[  72]                     </Pressable>
[  73]                 </View>
[  74] 
[  75]                 <Pressable 
[  76]                     style={[styles.doneButton, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}
[  77]                     onPress={() => onComplete(rotations)}
[  78]                 >
[  79]                     <Text style={styles.doneButtonText}>Terminer</Text>
[  80]                     <Ionicons name="arrow-forward" size={16} color="#fff" style={{marginLeft: 4}} />
[  81]                 </Pressable>
[  82]             </View>
[  83] 
[  84]             {/* PREVIEW AREA */}
[  85]             <ScrollView 
[  86]                 style={styles.scrollArea} 
[  87]                 contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
[  88]             >
[  89]                 {pages.map((page, index) => (
[  90]                     <View key={index} style={styles.pageWrapper}>
[  91]                         <View style={[styles.pageContainer, { borderColor: colors.border }]}>
[  92]                             {/* Inner container handles the rotation so the outer container stays the same size */}
[  93]                             <View style={{ width: 210, height: 297, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
[  94]                                 <Image
[  95]                                     source={{ uri: page }}
[  96]                                     style={[
[  97]                                         styles.pageImage,
[  98]                                         { transform: [{ rotate: rotations[index] + 'deg' }] }
[  99]                                     ]}
[ 100]                                     resizeMode="contain"
[ 101]                                 />
[ 102]                             </View>
[ 103]                             
[ 104]                             {/* Circular Rotate Button overlay */}
[ 105]                             <Pressable 
[ 106]                                 style={[styles.rotateButton, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}
[ 107]                                 onPress={() => rotatePage(index)}
[ 108]                             >
[ 109]                                 <Ionicons name="refresh" size={24} color="#fff" />
[ 110]                             </Pressable>
[ 111]                         </View>
[ 112]                         <Text style={[styles.pageNumber, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>Page {index + 1}</Text>
[ 113]                     </View>

[   1] import { Href, Link } from 'expo-router';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   2] import { openBrowserAsync, WebBrowserPresentationStyle } from 'expo-web-browser';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   3] import { type ComponentProps } from 'react';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   4] 
[   5] type Props = Omit<ComponentProps<typeof Link>, 'href'> & { href: Href & string };
[   6] 
[   7] export function ExternalLink({ href, ...rest }: Props) {
[   8]   return (
[   9]     <Link
[  10]       target="_blank"
[  11]       {...rest}
[  12]       href={href}
[  13]       onPress={async (event) => {
[  14]         if (process.env.EXPO_OS !== 'web') {
[  15]           // Prevent the default behavior of linking to the default browser on native.
[  16]           event.preventDefault();
[  17]           // Open the link in an in-app browser.
[  18]           await openBrowserAsync(href, {
[  19]             presentationStyle: WebBrowserPresentationStyle.AUTOMATIC,
[  20]           });
[  21]         }
[  22]       }}
[  23]     />
[  24]   );
[  25] }

[  22]                     </LinearGradient>

[  46]                 )}
[  47]

[  56]

[  25]     onSave: (signature: SignatureData) => void;
[  26]     colors: any;
[  27] }
[  28] 
[  29] const STORAGE_KEY = 'faas_saved_signatures';
[  30] 
[  31] export default function SignaturePad({ visible, onClose, onSave, colors }: SignaturePadProps) {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Déclaration du composant UI principal de ce fichier.
[  32]     const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'draw' | 'type' | 'upload'>('draw');
[  33]     
[  34]     // Draw state
[  35]     const [paths, setPaths] = useState<string[]>([]);
[  36]     const [currentPath, setCurrentPath] = useState<string>('');
[  37]     const [canvasSize, setCanvasSize] = useState({ width: 0, height: 0 });
[  38]     
[  39]     // Type state
[  40]     const [typedText, setTypedText] = useState('');
[  41] 
[  42]     // Saved signatures
[  43]     const [savedSignatures, setSavedSignatures] = useState<SignatureData[]>([]);
[  44] 
[  45]     useEffect(() => {
[  46]         loadSavedSignatures();
[  47]     }, []);
[  48] 
[  49]     const loadSavedSignatures = async () => {
[  50]         try {
[  51]             const saved = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
[  52]             if (saved) {
[  53]                 setSavedSignatures(JSON.parse(saved));
[  54]             }
[  55]         } catch (e) {
[  56]             console.error("Error loading signatures", e);
[  57]         }
[  58]     };
[  59] 
[  60]     const saveSignatureToStorage = async (sig: SignatureData) => {
[  61]         try {
[  62]             const newSaved = [sig, ...savedSignatures].slice(0, 5); // Keep last 5
[  63]             await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(newSaved));
[  64]             setSavedSignatures(newSaved);
[  65]         } catch (e) {
[  66]             console.error("Error saving signature", e);
[  67]         }
[  68]     };
[  69] 
[  70]     const currentPathRef = useRef('');
[  71] 
[  72]     const panResponder = useRef(
[  73]         PanResponder.create({
[  74]             onStartShouldSetPanResponder: () => true,
[  75]             onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: () => true,
[  76]             onPanResponderGrant: (e) => {
[  77]                 const { locationX, locationY } = e.nativeEvent;
[  78]                 const x = locationX ?? (e.nativeEvent as any).offsetX ?? 0;
[  79]                 const y = locationY ?? (e.nativeEvent as any).offsetY ?? 0;
[  80]                 currentPathRef.current = `M${x},${y}`;
[  81]                 setCurrentPath(currentPathRef.current);
[  82]             },
[  83]             onPanResponderMove: (e) => {
[  84]                 const { locationX, locationY } = e.nativeEvent;
[  85]                 const x = locationX ?? (e.nativeEvent as any).offsetX ?? 0;
[  86]                 const y = locationY ?? (e.nativeEvent as any).offsetY ?? 0;
[  87]                 currentPathRef.current += ` L${x},${y}`;
[  88]                 setCurrentPath(currentPathRef.current);
[  89]             },
[  90]             onPanResponderRelease: () => {
[  91]                 const finishedPath = currentPathRef.current;
[  92]                 setPaths(prev => [...prev, finishedPath]);
[  93]                 currentPathRef.current = '';
[  94]                 setCurrentPath('');
[  95]             }
[  96]         })
[  97]     ).current;
[  98] 
[  99]     const clearDraw = () => {
[ 100]         setPaths([]);
[ 101]         currentPathRef.current = '';
[ 102]         setCurrentPath('');
[ 103]     };
[ 104] 
[ 105]     const handleUpload = async () => {
[ 106]         try {
[ 107]             const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
[ 108]                 mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
[ 109]                 allowsEditing: true,
[ 110]                 quality: 1,
[ 111]                 base64: true,
[ 112]             });
[ 113] 
[ 114]             if (!result.canceled && result.assets && result.assets.length > 0) {
[ 115]                 const base64 = result.assets[0].base64;
[ 116]                 const uri = result.assets[0].uri;
[ 117]                 const imageStr = base64 ? `data:image/jpeg;base64,${base64}` : uri;
[ 118]                 
[ 119]                 const sig: SignatureData = { type: 'image', data: imageStr };
[ 120]                 await saveSignatureToStorage(sig);
[ 121]                 onSave(sig);
[ 122]                 onClose();
[ 123]             }
[ 124]         } catch (e) {
[ 125]             console.error("Image pick error", e);
[ 126]         }
[ 127]     };
[ 128] 
[ 129]     const handleSaveDraw = () => {
[ 130]         if (paths.length === 0 && !currentPath) return;
[ 131]         const allPaths = [...paths, currentPath].filter(p => p.length > 0).join(' ');
[ 132]         const sig: SignatureData = { type: 'path', data: allPaths, width: canvasSize.width, height: canvasSize.height };
[ 133]         saveSignatureToStorage(sig);
[ 134]         onSave(sig);
[ 135]         onClose();
[ 136]         clearDraw();
[ 137]     };
[ 138] 
[ 139]     const handleSaveType = () => {
[ 140]         if (!typedText.trim()) return;
[ 141]         const sig: SignatureData = { type: 'text', data: typedText.trim() };
[ 142]         saveSignatureToStorage(sig);
[ 143]         onSave(sig);
[ 144]         onClose();
[ 145]         setTypedText('');
[ 146]     };
[ 147] 
[ 148]     const renderSavedSignatures = () => {
[ 149]         if (savedSignatures.length === 0) return null;
[ 150]         return (
[ 151]             <View style={{ marginTop: 20 }}>
[ 152]                 <Text style={{ color: colors.textMuted, marginBottom: 8, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 'bold', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
[ 153]                     Signatures Sauvegardées
[ 154]                 </Text>
[ 155]                 <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 10 }}>
[ 156]                     {savedSignatures.map((sig, i) => (
[ 157]                         <Pressable 
[ 158]                             key={i} 
[ 159]                             style={[styles.savedSigBox, { borderColor: colors.border, backgroundColor: colors.card }]}
[ 160]                             onPress={() => {
[ 161]                                 onSave(sig);
[ 162]                                 onClose();
[ 163]                             }}
[ 164]                         >
[ 165]                             {sig.type === 'text' && (
[ 166]                                 <Text style={{ fontFamily: 'serif', fontStyle: 'italic', fontSize: 18, color: colors.text }}>
[ 167]                                     {sig.data}
[ 168]                                 </Text>
[ 169]                             )}
[ 170]                             {sig.type === 'path' && (
[ 171]                                 <Svg width={80} height={40} viewBox={`0 0 ${sig.width} ${sig.height}`}>
[ 172]                                     <Path d={sig.data} stroke={colors.text} strokeWidth={4} fill="none" />
[ 173]                                 </Svg>
[ 174]                             )}
[ 175]                             {sig.type === 'image' && (
[ 176]                                 <View style={{ width: 80, height: 40, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: '#eee', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
[ 177]                                     <Ionicons name="image" size={24} color={colors.textMuted} style={{ alignSelf: 'center', marginTop: 8 }} />
[ 178]                                 </View>
[ 179]                             )}
[ 180]                         </Pressable>
[ 181]                     ))}
[ 182]                 </View>
[ 183]             </View>
[ 184]         );
[ 185]     };
[ 186] 
[ 187]     return (
[ 188]         <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="slide">
[ 189]             <View style={styles.overlay}>
[ 190]                 <View style={[styles.modalContent, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
[ 191]                     
[ 192]                     <View style={[styles.header, { borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
[ 193]                         <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.text }]}>Créer une signature</Text>
[ 194]                         <Pressable onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeBtn}>
[ 195]                             <Ionicons name="close" size={24} color={colors.text} />
[ 196]                         </Pressable>
[ 197]                     </View>
[ 198] 
[ 199]                     <View style={styles.tabs}>
[ 200]                         <Pressable style={[styles.tab, activeTab === 'draw' && styles.tabActive]} onPress={() => setActiveTab('draw')}>
[ 201]                             <Text style={[styles.tabText, { color: activeTab === 'draw' ? '#3498db' : colors.textMuted }]}>Dessiner</Text>
[ 202]                         </Pressable>
[ 203]                         <Pressable style={[styles.tab, activeTab === 'type' && styles.tabActive]} onPress={() => setActiveTab('type')}>
[ 204]                             <Text style={[styles.tabText, { color: activeTab === 'type' ? '#3498db' : colors.textMuted }]}>Taper</Text>
[ 205]                         </Pressable>
[ 206]                         <Pressable style={[styles.tab, activeTab === 'upload' && styles.tabActive]} onPress={() => setActiveTab('upload')}>
[ 207]                             <Text style={[styles.tabText, { color: activeTab === 'upload' ? '#3498db' : colors.textMuted }]}>Importer</Text>
[ 208]                         </Pressable>
[ 209]                     </View>
[ 210] 
[ 211]                     <View style={styles.contentArea}>
[ 212]                         {activeTab === 'draw' && (
[ 213]                             <View style={styles.drawContainer}>
[ 214]                                 <View 
[ 215]                                     style={[styles.canvasArea, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.03)', borderColor: colors.border }]}
[ 216]                                     onLayout={(e) => setCanvasSize({ width: e.nativeEvent.layout.width, height: e.nativeEvent.layout.height })}
[ 217]                                     {...panResponder.panHandlers}
[ 218]                                 >
[ 219]                                     <Svg width="100%" height="100%">
[ 220]                                         {paths.map((p, i) => (
[ 221]                                             <Path key={i} d={p} stroke={colors.text} strokeWidth={4} fill="none" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />
[ 222]                                         ))}
[ 223]                                         {currentPath ? (
[ 224]                                             <Path d={currentPath} stroke={colors.text} strokeWidth={4} fill="none" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />
[ 225]                                         ) : null}
[ 226]                                     </Svg>
[ 227]                                 </View>
[ 228]                                 <View style={styles.drawActions}>

[ 235]                                 </View>
[ 236]                             </View>

[ 238] 
[ 239]                         {activeTab === 'type' && (

[ 243]                                     placeholder="Votre Nom"
[ 244]                                     placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}

[ 248]                                 />

--- FICHIER : components/PdfThumbnail.tsx (16 lignes) ---

[   1] import React from 'react';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.

        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.

--- FICHIER : components/CompressionSelector.tsx (170 lignes) ---

[   1] import React from 'react';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.
[   2] import { View, Text, Pressable, Platform } from 'react-native';
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.

        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.

        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Importation d'un module clé. Essentiel pour les dépendances du composant.

[   6] export type CompressionLevel = 'low' | 'recommended' | 'extreme';
[   7] 
[   8] interface CompressionSelectorProps {
[   9]     value: CompressionLevel;
[  10]     onChange: (val: CompressionLevel) => void;
[  11]     fileSize?: number; // Taille originale en octets
[  12] }
[  13] 
[  14] const LEVEL_CONFIG = {
[  15]     low: {
[  16]         title: 'Simple',
[  17]         subtitle: 'Haute qualité. Idéal pour l\'impression.',
[  18]         reduction: 40,
[  19]         pro: false
[  20]     },
[  21]     recommended: {
[  22]         title: 'Modéré',
[  23]         subtitle: 'Le meilleur équilibre. Parfait pour le web.',
[  24]         reduction: 75,
[  25]         pro: true
[  26]     },
[  27]     extreme: {
[  28]         title: 'Fort',
[  29]         subtitle: 'Qualité réduite. Taille minuscule.',
[  30]         reduction: 90,
[  31]         badge: 'Minimal',
[  32]         pro: true
[  33]     }
[  34] };
[  35] 
[  36] export default function CompressionSelector({ value, onChange, fileSize = 0 }: CompressionSelectorProps) {
        // ARCHITECTURE DOC: Déclaration du composant UI principal de ce fichier.
[  37]     const { colors } = useTheme();
[  38] 
[  39]     const formatSize = (bytes: number) => {
[  40]         if (!bytes || bytes === 0) return '0 Ko';
[  41]         const mb = bytes / (1024 * 1024);
[  42]         if (mb < 1) {
[  43]             const kb = bytes / 1024;
[  44]             return `${kb.toFixed(0)} Ko`;
[  45]         }
[  46]         return `${mb.toFixed(1)} Mo`;
[  47]     };
[  48] 
[  49]     const currentConfig = LEVEL_CONFIG[value];
[  50]     const targetNewSize = fileSize * (1 - currentConfig.reduction / 100);
[  51]     const targetSavedSize = fileSize - targetNewSize;
[  52] 
[  53]     const [animNewSize, setAnimNewSize] = React.useState(targetNewSize);
[  54]     const [animSavedSize, setAnimSavedSize] = React.useState(targetSavedSize);
[  55] 
[  56]     React.useEffect(() => {
[  57]         const steps = 15;
[  58]         let currentStep = 0;
[  59]         const diffNew = (targetNewSize - animNewSize) / steps;
[  60]         const diffSaved = (targetSavedSize - animSavedSize) / steps;
[  61] 
[  62]         const interval = setInterval(() => {
[  63]             currentStep++;
[  64]             setAnimNewSize(prev => prev + diffNew);
[  65]             setAnimSavedSize(prev => prev + diffSaved);
[  66]             if (currentStep >= steps) {
[  67]                 clearInterval(interval);
[  68]                 setAnimNewSize(targetNewSize);
[  69]                 setAnimSavedSize(targetSavedSize);
[  70]             }
[  71]         }, 20);
[  72] 
[  73]         return () => clearInterval(interval);
[  74]     }, [targetNewSize, targetSavedSize]);
[  75] 
[  76]     return (
[  77]         <View style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 400, alignSelf: 'center' }}>
[  78]             <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }}>
[  79]                 <View style={{ width: 32, height: 32, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', marginRight: 12 }}>
[  80]                     <Ionicons name="contract" size={18} color="#ef4444" />
[  81]                 </View>
[  82]                 <Text style={{ color: colors.text, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 'bold' }}>Compresser</Text>
[  83]             </View>
[  84]             
[  85]             <View style={{ gap: 12, marginBottom: 24 }}>
[  86]                 {(Object.keys(LEVEL_CONFIG) as CompressionLevel[]).map((levelKey) => {
[  87]                     const isSelected = value === levelKey;
[  88]                     const config = LEVEL_CONFIG[levelKey];
[  89] 
[  90]                     return (
[  91]                         <Pressable
[  92]                             key={levelKey}
[  93]                             onPress={() => onChange(levelKey)}
[  94]                             style={({ pressed, hovered }: any) => [
[  95]                                 {
[  96]                                     flexDirection: 'row',
[  97]                                     alignItems: 'center',
[  98]                                     justifyContent: 'space-between',
[  99]                                     backgroundColor: isSelected ? 'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.08)' : 'transparent',
[ 100]                                     borderWidth: 1,
[ 101]                                     borderColor: isSelected ? '#2563eb' : colors.border,
[ 102]                                     borderRadius: 8,
[ 103]                                     paddingVertical: 14,
[ 104]                                     paddingHorizontal: 16,
[ 105]                                     transform: [{ scale: pressed ? 0.98 : 1 }],
[ 106]                                     transition: 'all 0.15s ease'
[ 107]                                 },
[ 108]                                 hovered && !isSelected && { borderColor: colors.textMuted }
[ 109]                             ]}
[ 110]                         >
[ 111]                             <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
[ 112]                                 <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}>
[ 113]                                     <Ionicons 
[ 114]                                         name={isSelected ? "radio-button-on" : "radio-button-off"} 
[ 115]                                         size={22} 
[ 116]                                         color={isSelected ? '#2563eb' : colors.textSubtle} 
[ 117]                                         style={{ marginRight: 12 }}
[ 118]                                     />
[ 119]                                     <Text style={{ color: colors.text, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: isSelected ? '600' : '500' }}>
[ 120]                                         {config.title}
[ 121]                                     </Text>
[ 122]                                 </View>
[ 123]                                 {isSelected && (
[ 124]                                     <Text style={{ color: colors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, marginLeft: 34, marginTop: 4 }}>
[ 125]                                         {config.subtitle}
[ 126]                                     </Text>
[ 127]                                 )}
[ 128]                             </View>
[ 129] 
[ 130]                             <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
[ 131]                                 {config.badge && (
[ 132]                                     <Text style={{ color: colors.success, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 'bold', backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1)', paddingHorizontal: 6, paddingVertical: 2, borderRadius: 4 }}>
[ 133]                                         {config.badge}
[ 134]                                     </Text>
[ 135]                                 )}
[ 136]                                 {config.pro && (
[ 137]                                     <View style={{ backgroundColor: '#f59e0b', borderRadius: 10, width: 20, height: 20, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
[ 138]                                         <Ionicons name="star" size={12} color="#fff" />
[ 139]                                     </View>
[ 140]                                 )}
[ 141]                             </View>
[ 142]                         </Pressable>
[ 143]                     );
[ 144]                 })}
[ 145]             </View>
[ 146] 
[ 147]             {fileSize > 0 && (
[ 148]                 <View style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.08)', borderRadius: 8, padding: 16, marginBottom: 16 }}>
[ 149]                     <Text style={{ color: colors.text, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '500', marginBottom: 8 }}>
[ 150]                         Nouvelle taille de fichier estimée
[ 151]                     </Text>
[ 152]                     <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'flex-end', marginBottom: 12 }}>
[ 153]                         <Text style={{ color: colors.success, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
[ 154]                             -{formatSize(animSavedSize)}
[ 155]                         </Text>
[ 156]                         <Text style={{ color: colors.textMuted, fontSize: 13, marginLeft: 8, paddingBottom: 2 }}>
[ 157]                             ~{formatSize(animNewSize)} (-{currentConfig.reduction}%)
[ 158]                         </Text>
[ 159]                     </View>
[ 160]                     
[ 161]                     {/* Progress Bar Visual */}
[ 162]                     <View style={{ height: 4, backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2)', borderRadius: 2, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
[ 163]                         <View style={{ height: '100%', width: `${100 - currentConfig.reduction}%`, backgroundColor: colors.success, borderRadius: 2 }} />
[ 164]                     </View>
[ 165]                 </View>
[ 166]             )}
[ 167]         </View>

[  12]

[  41]       })}

[  51]     const [dragOverPage, setDragOverPage] = useState<number | null>(null);
[  52] 
[  53]     const [draggedFile, setDraggedFile] = useState<number | null>(null);
[  54]     const [dragOverFile, setDragOverFile] = useState<number | null>(null);
[  55] 
[  56]     // --- DRAG & DROP FOR PAGES ---
[  57]     const onPageDragStart = (e: any, index: number) => {
[  58]         setDraggedPage(index);
[  59]         const dataTransfer = e.nativeEvent?.dataTransfer || e.dataTransfer;
[  60]         if (dataTransfer) {
[  61]             dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
[  62]             dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', index.toString());
[  63]         }
[  64]     };
[  65] 
[  66]     const onPageDragOver = (e: any, index: number) => {
[  67]         e.preventDefault();
[  68]         setDragOverPage(index);
[  69]     };
[  70] 
[  71]     const onPageDrop = (e: any, targetIndex: number) => {
[  72]         e.preventDefault();
[  73]         setDragOverPage(null);
[  74]         if (draggedPage === null) return;
[  75]         if (draggedPage === targetIndex) return;
[  76] 
[  77]         setPages(prev => {
[  78]             const next = [...prev];
[  79]             const item = next.splice(draggedPage, 1)[0];
[  80]             next.splice(targetIndex, 0, item);
[  81]             return next;
[  82]         });
[  83]         setDraggedPage(null);
[  84]     };
[  85] 
[  86]     // --- DRAG & DROP FOR FILES (SIDEBAR) ---
[  87]     const onFileDragStart = (e: any, index: number) => {
[  88]         setDraggedFile(index);
[  89]         const dataTransfer = e.nativeEvent?.dataTransfer || e.dataTransfer;
[  90]         if (dataTransfer) {
[  91]             dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
[  92]             dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', index.toString());
[  93]         }
[  94]     };
[  95] 
[  96]     const onFileDragOver = (e: any, index: number) => {
[  97]         e.preventDefault();
[  98]         setDragOverFile(index);
[  99]     };
[ 100] 
[ 101]     const onFileDrop = (e: any, targetIndex: number) => {
[ 102]         e.preventDefault();
[ 103]         setDragOverFile(null);
[ 104]         if (draggedFile === null) return;
[ 105]         if (draggedFile === targetIndex) return;
[ 106] 
[ 107]         setFilesOrder(prev => {
[ 108]             const next = [...prev];
[ 109]             const item = next.splice(draggedFile, 1)[0];
[ 110]             next.splice(targetIndex, 0, item);
[ 111]             
[ 112]             reorderPagesByFiles(next);
[ 113]             
[ 114]             return next;
[ 115]         });
[ 116]         setDraggedFile(null);
[ 117]     };
[ 118] 
[ 119]     const reorderPagesByFiles = (newFilesOrder: OrganizeFileItem[]) => {
[ 120]         setPages(prev => {
[ 121]             const groupedPages = new Map<number, OrganizePageItem[]>();
[ 122]             prev.forEach(p => {
[ 123]                 if (!groupedPages.has(p.fileIndex)) {
[ 124]                     groupedPages.set(p.fileIndex, []);
[ 125]                 }
[ 126]                 groupedPages.get(p.fileIndex)!.push(p);
[ 127]             });
[ 128] 
[ 129]             const newPages: OrganizePageItem[] = [];
[ 130]             newFilesOrder.forEach(file => {
[ 131]                 const pagesForFile = groupedPages.get(file.originalIndex);
[ 132]                 if (pagesForFile) {
[ 133]                     newPages.push(...pagesForFile);
[ 134]                 }
[ 135]             });
[ 136]             return newPages;
[ 137]         });
[ 138]     };
[ 139] 
[ 140]     const removePage = (id: string) => {
[ 141]         setPages(prev => prev.filter(p => p.id !== id));
[ 142]     };
[ 143] 
[ 144]     return (
[ 145]         <SafeAreaView style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
[ 146]             {/* TOOLBAR */}
[ 147]             <View style={[styles.toolbar, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
[ 148]                 <Pressable onPress={onCancel} style={styles.backButton}>
[ 149]                     <Ionicons name="close" size={28} color={colors.text} />
[ 150]                 </Pressable>
[ 151] 
[ 152]                 <View style={styles.toolsContainer}>
[ 153]                     <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.text }]}>Organiser le PDF</Text>
[ 154]                 </View>
[ 155]             </View>
[ 156] 
[ 157]             <View style={styles.content}>
[ 158]                 {/* PREVIEW AREA */}
[ 159]                 <View style={styles.mainArea}>
[ 160]                     <View style={styles.addFileWrapper}>
[ 161]                         <Pressable 
[ 162]                             style={styles.addFileButton}
[ 163]                             onPress={onAddFiles}
[ 164]                         >
[ 165]                             <Ionicons name="add" size={32} color="#fff" />
[ 166]                         </Pressable>
[ 167]                     </View>
[ 168] 
[ 169]                     <ScrollView 
[ 170]                         style={styles.scrollArea} 
[ 171]                         contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
[ 172]                     >
[ 173]                         {pages.map((page, index) => {
[ 174]                             const fileInfo = filesOrder.find(f => f.originalIndex === page.fileIndex) || filesOrder[0];
[ 175]                             const isDragged = draggedPage === index;
[ 176]                             const isDragOver = dragOverPage === index;
[ 177] 
[ 178]                             const content = (
[ 179]                                 <>
[ 180]                                     <View style={[
[ 181]                                         styles.pageContainer, 
[ 182]                                         { borderColor: fileInfo.color }
[ 183]                                     ]}>
[ 184]                                         <Image
[ 185]                                             source={{ uri: page.imageUri }}
[ 186]                                             style={styles.pageImage}
[ 187]                                             resizeMode="contain"
[ 188]                                             pointerEvents="none"
[ 189]                                         />
[ 190]                                         
[ 191]                                         {/* Delete Button (iLovePDF style) */}
[ 192]                                         <Pressable 
[ 193]                                             style={styles.deleteButton}
[ 194]                                             onPress={() => removePage(page.id)}
[ 195]                                         >
[ 196]                                             <Ionicons name="close" size={12} color="#fff" />
[ 197]                                         </Pressable>
[ 198]                                     </View>
[ 199]                                     <Text style={[styles.pageNumber, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>{index + 1}</Text>
[ 200]                                 </>
[ 201]                             );
[ 202] 
[ 203]                             return Platform.OS === 'web' ? (
[ 204]                                 <div
[ 205]                                     key={page.id}
[ 206]                                     style={{
[ 207]                                         ...StyleSheet.flatten([
[ 208]                                             styles.pageWrapper,
[ 209]                                             isDragged && styles.pageWrapperDragging,
[ 210]                                             isDragOver && styles.pageWrapperDragOver,
[ 211]                                         ])
[ 212]                                     }}
[ 213]                                     draggable={true}
[ 214]                                     onDragStart={(e: any) => onPageDragStart(e, index)}
[ 215]                                     onDragOver={(e: any) => onPageDragOver(e, index)}
[ 216]                                     onDrop={(e: any) => onPageDrop(e, index)}
[ 217]                                     onDragEnd={() => { setDraggedPage(null); setDragOverPage(null); }}
[ 218]                                 >
[ 219]                                     {content}
[ 220]                                 </div>
[ 221]                             ) : (
[ 222]                                 <View 
[ 223]                                     key={page.id}
[ 224]                                     style={[
[ 225]                                         styles.pageWrapper,
[ 226]                                         isDragged && styles.pageWrapperDragging,
[ 227]                                         isDragOver && styles.pageWrapperDragOver,
[ 228]                                     ]}
[ 229]                                 >
[ 230]                                     {content}
[ 231]                                 </View>
[ 232]                             );
[ 233]                         })}
[ 234]                     </ScrollView>
[ 235]                 </View>
[ 236] 
[ 237]                 {/* SIDEBAR */}
[ 238]                 <View style={[styles.sidebar, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderLeftColor: colors.border }]}>
[ 239]                     <View style={styles.sidebarHeader}>
[ 240]                         <Text style={[styles.sidebarTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>Fichiers</Text>
[ 241]                     </View>
[ 242]                     
[ 243]                     <ScrollView style={styles.fileList}>
[ 244]                         {filesOrder.map((file, i) => {
[ 245]                             const count = pages.filter(p => p.fileIndex === file.originalIndex).length;
[ 246]                             const isDragged = draggedFile === i;
[ 247]                             const isDragOver = dragOverFile === i;
[ 248] 
[ 249]                             const content = (
[ 250]                                 <View style={[styles.fileItem, { backgroundColor: file.color }]}>
[ 251]                                     <View style={styles.fileIcon}>
[ 252]                                         <Ionicons name="swap-vertical" size={16} color="#333" />
[ 253]                                     </View>
[ 254]                                     <Text style={[styles.fileName, { color: '#333' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
[ 255]                                         {String.fromCharCode(65 + i)}: {file.name}
[ 256]                                     </Text>
[ 257]                                     <Text style={[styles.pageCount, { color: '#555' }]}>
[ 258]                                         ({count})
[ 259]                                     </Text>
[ 260]                                 </View>
[ 261]                             );
[ 262] 
[ 263]                             return Platform.OS === 'web' ? (
[ 264]                                 <div 
[ 265]                                     key={file.originalIndex} 
[ 266]                                     style={{
[ 267]                                         ...StyleSheet.flatten([
[ 268]                                             styles.fileItemWrapper,
[ 269]                                             isDragged && styles.fileItemDragging,
[ 270]                                             isDragOver && styles.fileItemDragOver
[ 271]                                         ])
[ 272]                                     }}
[ 273]                                     draggable={true}
[ 274]                                     onDragStart={(e: any) => onFileDragStart(e, i)}
[ 275]                                     onDragOver={(e: any) => onFileDragOver(e, i)}
[ 276]                                     onDrop={(e: any) => onFileDrop(e, i)}
[ 277]                                     onDragEnd={() => { setDraggedFile(null); setDragOverFile(null); }}
[ 278]                                 >
[ 279]                                     {content}
[ 280]                                 </div>

[ 282]                                 <View 
[ 283]                                     key={file.originalIndex} 
[ 284]                                     style={[
[ 285]                                         styles.fileItemWrapper,
[ 286]                                         isDragged && styles.fileItemDragging,
[ 287]                                         isDragOver && styles.fileItemDragOver
[ 288]                                     ]}
[ 289]                                 >
[ 290]                                     {content}
[ 291]                                 </View>
[ 292]                             );
[ 293]                         })}
[ 294]                     </ScrollView>
[ 295] 
[ 296]                     <Pressable 
[ 297]                         style={[styles.doneButton, { backgroundColor: '#e74c3c' }]}

[ 299]                     >
[ 300]                         <Text style={styles.doneButtonText}>Organiser</Text>
[ 301]                         <Ionicons name="arrow-forward" size={16} color="#fff" style={{marginLeft: 4}} />
[ 302]                     </Pressable>
[ 303]                 </View>
[ 304]             </View>
[ 305]         </SafeAreaView>
[ 306]     );
[ 307] }

[ 166]         fontWeight: 'bold',
[ 167]     }
[ 168] });

[  20] const DraggableItem = ({ x, y, canvasSize, onDragEnd, isSelected, onSelect, children }: any) => {
[  21]     const pan = useRef(new Animated.ValueXY({ x: 0, y: 0 })).current;
[  22] 
[  23]     // Use refs to keep latest values and avoid stale closures in PanResponder
[  24]     const posRef = useRef({ x, y });
[  25]     const dragEndRef = useRef(onDragEnd);
[  26] 
[  27]     useEffect(() => {
[  28]         posRef.current = { x, y };
[  29]         dragEndRef.current = onDragEnd;
[  30]     }, [x, y, onDragEnd]);
[  31] 
[  32]     const panResponder = useRef(
[  33]         PanResponder.create({
[  34]             onStartShouldSetPanResponder: () => true,
[  35]             onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: () => true,
[  36]             onPanResponderGrant: () => {
[  37]                 if (onSelect) onSelect();
[  38]             },
[  39]             onPanResponderMove: Animated.event([null, { dx: pan.x, dy: pan.y }], { useNativeDriver: false }),
[  40]             onPanResponderRelease: (e, gestureState) => {
[  41]                 const percentDx = (gestureState.dx / canvasSize.width) * 100;
[  42]                 const percentDy = (gestureState.dy / canvasSize.height) * 100;
[  43]                 dragEndRef.current(posRef.current.x + percentDx, posRef.current.y + percentDy);
[  44]                 pan.setValue({ x: 0, y: 0 });
[  45]             }
[  46]         })
[  47]     ).current;
[  48] 
[  49]     return (
[  50]         <Animated.View
[  51]             style={{
[  52]                 position: 'absolute',
[  53]                 left: `${x}%`,
[  54]                 top: `${y}%`,
[  55]                 transform: [{ translateX: pan.x }, { translateY: pan.y }],
[  56]                 flexDirection: 'column',
[  57]                 zIndex: isSelected ? 1000 : 100,
[  58]             }}
[  59]         >
[  60]             {children(panResponder.panHandlers)}
[  61]         </Animated.View>
[  62]     );
[  63] };
[  64]

[  69]     pageIndex: number;
[  70]     type: PdfEditType;
[  71]     x: number; // percentage 0-100
[  72]     y: number; // percentage 0-100
[  73]     width?: number; // for replace background
[  74]     height?: number; // for replace background
[  75]     backgroundColor?: string; // for replace background

[  77]     color?: string;

[  79]     fontWeight?: 'normal' | 'bold';
[  80]     fontStyle?: 'normal' | 'italic';
[  81]     textAlign?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right';

[ 116]     // For handling dragging and temporary text

[ 119]     // Sync formatting states when an edit is selected
[ 120]     useEffect(() => {
[ 121]         if (selectedEditId) {
[ 122]             const edit = edits.find(e => e.id === selectedEditId);
[ 123]             if (edit) {
[ 124]                 if (edit.color) setSelectedColor(edit.color);

[ 126]                 if (edit.fontWeight) setSelectedWeight(edit.fontWeight);

[ 133]     // Update formatting for new items AND the currently selected item
[ 134]     const updateFormatting = (updates: Partial<PdfEditItem>) => {
[ 135]         if (updates.color) setSelectedColor(updates.color);
[ 136]         if (updates.size) setSelectedSize(updates.size);
[ 137]         if (updates.fontWeight) setSelectedWeight(updates.fontWeight);
[ 138]         if (updates.fontStyle) setSelectedStyle(updates.fontStyle);
[ 139]         if (updates.textAlign) setSelectedAlign(updates.textAlign);
[ 140]         
[ 141]         if (selectedEditId) {
[ 142]             setEdits(prev => prev.map(e => e.id === selectedEditId ? { ...e, ...updates } : e));
[ 143]         }
[ 144]     };

[ 146]     // Nettoyage des textes vides lors de la désélection
[ 147]     useEffect(() => {
[ 148]         setEdits(prev => prev.filter(e => e.type !== 'text' || e.text?.trim() !== '' || e.id === selectedEditId));
[ 149]     }, [selectedEditId]);

[ 151]     const handleCanvasPress = (e: any) => {
[ 152]         if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 153]             const nativeEvent = e.nativeEvent as any;
[ 154]             const targetId = nativeEvent.target?.id;
[ 155]             // Ne pas traiter le clic si on a cliqué sur un élément enfant (comme un champ texte ou un bouton)
[ 156]             if (targetId !== 'pdf-canvas-overlay') {
[ 157]                 return;
[ 158]             }
[ 159]         }
[ 160] 
[ 161]         if (selectedEditId) {
[ 162]             setSelectedEditId(null);
[ 163]         }
[ 164] 
[ 165]         if (activeTool !== 'text' && activeTool !== 'replace') return;
[ 166]         
[ 167]         // Use nativeEvent for cross-platform local coordinates
[ 168]         // Cross-platform coordinate calculation
[ 169]         let x = 0;
[ 170]         let y = 0;
[ 171] 
[ 172]         if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
[ 173]             // Sur le web, e.nativeEvent.offsetX/offsetY est le plus fiable
[ 174]             const nativeEvent = e.nativeEvent as any;
[ 175]             if (nativeEvent.offsetX !== undefined) {
[ 176]                 x = (nativeEvent.offsetX / canvasSize.width) * 100;
[ 177]                 y = (nativeEvent.offsetY / canvasSize.height) * 100;
[ 178]             } else {
[ 179]                 // Fallback avec getBoundingClientRect
[ 180]                 const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
[ 181]                 const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
[ 182]                 x = ((nativeEvent.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 100;
[ 183]                 y = ((nativeEvent.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 100;
[ 184]             }
[ 185]         } else {
[ 186]             // Sur mobile natif
[ 187]             const { locationX, locationY } = e.nativeEvent;
[ 188]             x = (locationX / canvasSize.width) * 100;
[ 189]             y = (locationY / canvasSize.height) * 100;
[ 190]         }
[ 191]         
[ 192]         if (activeTool === 'replace') {
[ 193]             const newEdit: PdfEditItem = {
[ 194]                 id: Date.now().toString(),
[ 195]                 pageIndex: currentPageIndex,
[ 196]                 type: 'text',
[ 197]                 x, y,
[ 198]                 width: 15,
[ 199]                 height: 4,
[ 200]                 backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
[ 201]                 text: '',
[ 202]                 color: selectedColor,
[ 203]                 size: selectedSize,
[ 204]                 fontWeight: selectedWeight,
[ 205]                 fontStyle: selectedStyle,
[ 206]                 textAlign: selectedAlign,
[ 207]             };
[ 208]             setEdits([...edits, newEdit]);
[ 209]             setSelectedEditId(newEdit.id);
[ 210]             return;
[ 211]         }
[ 212] 
[ 213]         if (activeTool === 'text') {
[ 214]             const newEdit: PdfEditItem = {
[ 215]                 id: Date.now().toString(),
[ 216]                 pageIndex: currentPageIndex,
[ 217]                 type: 'text',
[ 218]                 x,
[ 219]                 y,
[ 220]                 text: '',
[ 221]                 color: selectedColor,

[ 224]                 fontStyle: selectedStyle,
[ 225]                 textAlign: selectedAlign,
[ 226]             };
